                        }
                        g.depthMask(true);
                        g.bindVertexArray(null);
                    }
                    dispose() {
                        const g = this.gl;
                        this.resources.forEach(b => g.deleteBuffer(b));
                        [this.cubeP, this.lineP, this.pointP].forEach(p => g.deleteProgram(p));
                        [this.cubeVAO, this.lineVAO, this.pointVAO].forEach(v => g.deleteVertexArray(v));
                    }
                }
                class AtlasEngine {
                    constructor(canvas, labelRoot, tip, callbacks, state) {
                        this.canvas = canvas;
                        this.labelRoot = labelRoot;
                        this.tip = tip;
                        this.cb = callbacks;
                        this.state = state;
                        try {
                            this.renderer = new GLRenderer(canvas);
                        } catch (error) {
                            this.renderer = new CanvasRenderer(canvas);
                            this.fallbackReason = error.message;
                        }
                        this.items = new Map;
                        this.expands = new Map;
                        this.time = state.time || 0;
                        this.clock = this.time;
                        this.last = performance.now();
                        this.labelPool = [];
                        this.hover = null;
                        this.lastTick = 0;
                        this.phaseIndex = 0;
                        this.frameNo = 0;
                        this.deck = state.layout === 'split' ? 1 : 0;
                        this.reduced = matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
                        this.cam = {
                            theta: .34,
                            phi: 1.11,
                            dist: 49,
                            target: [-3, 7.8, 0]
                        };
                        this.to = {
                            ...this.cam,
                            target: [...this.cam.target]
                        };
                        this.lastView = state.view;
                        this.fov = 42 * Math.PI / 180;
                        this.disposers = [];
                        this.pointers = new Map;
                        this.mx = -1;
                        this.my = -1;
                        this.bind();
                        this.ro = new ResizeObserver( () => this.resize());
                        this.ro.observe(canvas.parentElement);
                        this.resize();
                        this.reset(true);
                        this.loop = this.loop.bind(this);
                        this.raf = requestAnimationFrame(this.loop);
                    }
                    resize() {
                        const r = this.canvas.parentElement.getBoundingClientRect();
                        this.w = Math.max(1, r.width);
                        this.h = Math.max(1, r.height);
                        this.renderer.resize(this.w, this.h);
                        if (this.layoutCache)
                            this.layoutCache.clear();
                        if (this.state.selected && this.state.view === 'architecture')
                            this.focus(this.state.selected);
                        else if (this.to)
                            this.reset();
                    }
                    sync(s) {
                        const old = this.state;
                        this.state = s;
                        if (old.view !== s.view) {
                            this.lastView = s.view;
                            this.items.clear();
                            this.expands.clear();
                            this.hover = null;
                            this.reset();
                        }
                        if (old.fontScale !== s.fontScale && this.layoutCache)
                            this.layoutCache.clear();
                        if (s.view === 'architecture' && s.selected && (old.selected !== s.selected || old.detailLayout !== s.detailLayout || old.expertView !== s.expertView || old.precision !== s.precision || old.layout !== s.layout || old.fontScale !== s.fontScale))
                            this.focus(s.selected);
                        if (s.view === 'architecture' && ((old.selected && !s.selected) || old.layout !== s.layout || old.fontScale !== s.fontScale) && !s.selected)
                            this.reset();
                    }
                    reset(instant=false) {
                        const view = this.state.view
                          , wide = this.w / Math.max(1, this.h)
                          , f = Math.tan(this.fov / 2)
                          , reserve = Math.max(1.1, this.h / Math.max(150, this.h - 115))
                          , split = this.state.layout === 'split';
                        let dest = view === 'storage' ? {
                            theta: .37,
                            phi: 1.12,
                            dist: wide < 1 ? 58 : 40,
                            target: [-1.7, 2.2, 0]
                        } : view === 'cache' ? {
                            theta: .1,
                            phi: 1.3,
                            dist: wide < 1 ? 16 : 11.6,
                            target: [0, .25, 0]
                        } : {
                            theta: .30,
                            phi: 1.25,
                            dist: Math.max((split ? 25 : 35) / (2 * f) * reserve, (split ? 27 : 20) / (2 * f * wide) * 1.12),
                            target: split ? [-2.4, 8.5, 0] : [-1.8, 14.7, 0]
                        };
                        dest.dist = clamp(dest.dist, 10, 160);
                        this.to = dest;
                        if (instant)
                            this.cam = {
                                ...dest,
                                target: [...dest.target]
                            };
                    }

                    focus(id) {
                        const m = MODULES.find(x => x.id === id);
                        if (!m)
                            return;
                        const p = this.modulePos(m, this.state.layout === 'split' ? 1 : 0)
                          , plan = this.tensorLayout(m, this.state.precision)
                          , aspect = this.w / Math.max(1, this.h)
                          , f = Math.tan(this.fov / 2)
                          , reserve = this.h / Math.max(150, this.h - 155)
                          , floor = this.state.detailLayout === 'floor' && !plan.grid;
                        let height = floor ? plan.height * .9 + 3 : plan.height + 1
                          , width = plan.grid ? plan.width + 2 : floor ? plan.width + 2 : plan.width + 7;
                        let dist = Math.max(height / (2 * f) * reserve, width / (2 * f * aspect) * 1.08);
                        this.to = {
                            theta: floor ? .16 : .035,
                            phi: floor ? .30 : 1.43,
                            dist: clamp(dist, 9, 150),
                            target: [p[0], p[1] + (floor ? 0 : 1), p[2] + (floor ? 0 : 2.8)]
                        };
                    }
                    seek(t) {
                        this.time = clamp(t, 0, 21.999);
                        this.clock = this.time;
                        this.cb.tick(this.time);
                    }
                    modulePos(m, deck=this.deck) {
                        // The same module IDs travel between layer-order and functional layouts.
                        // Horizontal separation happens first, so the two decks never pass through each other.
                        let tower, split;
                        if (m.id === 'embed') {
                            tower = [0, -1.3, 0];
                            split = [-4.8, -1.3, 0];
                        } else if (m.id === 'head') {
                            tower = [0, 27.7, 0];
                            split = [5.4, 16.0, 0];
                        } else if (m.id === 'vision') {
                            tower = [-7, -1.15, 1.5];
                            split = [-10, -1.1, 1.5];
                        } else if (m.id === 'aligner') {
                            tower = [-3.8, -1.15, 1.5];
                            split = [-7, -1.1, 1.5];
                        } else if (m.id[0] === 'E') {
                            tower = [-8.0, m.index * .67, -4.6];
                            split = [-12.3, m.index === 1 ? 3.2 : 10.5, -3.5];
                        } else if (m.id[0] === 'D') {
                            tower = [0, 29.1 + (m.index - 40) * .73, 0];
                            split = [5.4, 17.6 + (m.index - 40) * .73, 0];
                        } else {
                            tower = [0, m.index * .67, 0];
                            split = [m.index < 20 ? -4.8 : 5.4, (m.index % 20) * .75, 0];
                        }
                        const index = m.mode ? m.index % 20 : 0
                          , delay = index / 19 * .10
                          , u = clamp((deck - delay) / (.9), 0, 1)
                          , x = smooth(clamp(u / .48, 0, 1))
                          , y = smooth(clamp((u - .35) / .65, 0, 1));
                        return [lerp(tower[0], split[0], x), lerp(tower[1], split[1], y), lerp(tower[2], split[2], x)];
                    }

                    on(el, type, fn, opts) {
                        el.addEventListener(type, fn, opts);
                        this.disposers.push( () => el.removeEventListener(type, fn, opts));
                    }
                    bind() {
                        let c = this.canvas;
                        this.on(c, 'contextmenu', e => e.preventDefault());
                        this.on(c, 'pointerdown', e => {
                            e.preventDefault();
                            c.focus({
                                preventScroll: true
                            });
                            c.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
                            this.pointers.set(e.pointerId, {
                                x: e.clientX,
                                y: e.clientY
                            });
                            this.drag = {
                                x: e.clientX,
                                y: e.clientY,
                                startX: e.clientX,
                                startY: e.clientY,
                                moved: false,
                                pan: e.button === 1 || e.button === 2 || e.shiftKey,
                                touch: e.pointerType === 'touch'
                            };
                            if (this.pointers.size > 1)
                                this.drag.moved = true;
                        }
                        );
                        this.on(c, 'pointermove', e => {
                            let r = c.getBoundingClientRect();
                            this.mx = e.clientX - r.left;
                            this.my = e.clientY - r.top;
                            if (!this.pointers.has(e.pointerId))
                                return;
                            let previous = [...this.pointers.values()];
                            this.pointers.set(e.pointerId, {
                                x: e.clientX,
                                y: e.clientY
                            });
                            let current = [...this.pointers.values()];
                            if (current.length === 2) {
                                let pd = Math.hypot(previous[0].x - previous[1].x, previous[0].y - previous[1].y)
                                  , nd = Math.hypot(current[0].x - current[1].x, current[0].y - current[1].y);
                                if (nd > 1)
                                    this.to.dist = clamp(this.to.dist * pd / nd, 2.5, 200);
                                this.pan((current[0].x + current[1].x - previous[0].x - previous[1].x) / 2, (current[0].y + current[1].y - previous[0].y - previous[1].y) / 2);
                                if (this.drag)
                                    this.drag.moved = true;
                                return;
                            }
                            if (!this.drag)
                                return;
                            const d = this.drag
                              , dx = e.clientX - d.x
                              , dy = e.clientY - d.y;
                            if (Math.hypot(e.clientX - d.startX, e.clientY - d.startY) > 4)
                                d.moved = true;
                            if (d.pan)
                                this.pan(dx, dy);
                            else {
                                this.to.theta -= dx * .005;
                                this.to.phi = clamp(this.to.phi - dy * .005, .18, 2.86);
                            }
                            d.x = e.clientX;
                            d.y = e.clientY;
                        }
                        );
                        const end = e => {
                            let d = this.drag;
                            this.pointers.delete(e.pointerId);
                            if (this.pointers.size === 0) {
                                if (d && !d.moved && !d.pan && e.type !== 'pointercancel') {
                                    let r = c.getBoundingClientRect();
                                    const hit = this.pick(e.clientX - r.left, e.clientY - r.top);
                                    if (hit) {
                                        this.cb.select(hit.meta.module || null, hit.meta.tensor || null, hit.meta.expert);
                                    } else
                                        this.cb.select(null);
                                }
                                this.drag = null;
                            } else if (d) {
                                let p = [...this.pointers.values()][0];
                                d.x = p.x;
                                d.y = p.y;
                                d.moved = true;
                            }
                        }
                        ;
                        this.on(c, 'pointerup', end);
                        this.on(c, 'pointercancel', end);
                        this.on(c, 'lostpointercapture', () => {
                            if (!this.pointers.size)
                                this.drag = null;
                        }
                        );
                        this.on(c, 'pointerleave', () => {
                            if (!this.drag) {
                                this.mx = -1;
                                this.my = -1;
                                this.hover = null;
                                this.tip.style.display = 'none';
                            }
                        }
                        );
                        this.on(c, 'wheel', e => {
                            e.preventDefault();
                            this.to.dist = clamp(this.to.dist * Math.exp(clamp(e.deltaY, -200, 200) * .0013), 2.5, 200);
                        }
                        , {
                            passive: false
                        });
                        this.on(c, 'dblclick', () => this.reset());
                        this.on(c, 'webglcontextlost', e => {
                            e.preventDefault();
                            cancelAnimationFrame(this.raf);
                            this.cb.error('The graphics context was lost. Reload this file to restore the view.');
                        }
                        );
                    }
                    pan(dx, dy) {
                        const s = this.to.dist * 2 * Math.tan(this.fov / 2) / Math.max(1, this.h)
                          , th = this.to.theta
                          , ph = this.to.phi;
                        const right = [Math.cos(th), 0, -Math.sin(th)]
                          , up = [-Math.cos(ph) * Math.sin(th), Math.sin(ph), -Math.cos(ph) * Math.cos(th)];
                        this.to.target = this.to.target.map( (v, i) => v - dx * s * right[i] + dy * s * up[i]);
                    }
                    camera(dt) {
                        let a = this.reduced ? 1 : 1 - Math.exp(-dt * 9)
                          , c = this.cam
                          , t = this.to;
                        if (this.state.orbit && !this.drag && this.state.playing)
                            t.theta += dt * .06;
                        for (const k of ['theta', 'phi', 'dist'])
                            c[k] = lerp(c[k], t[k], a);
                        c.target = c.target.map( (v, i) => lerp(v, t.target[i], a));
                        const off = [c.dist * Math.sin(c.phi) * Math.sin(c.theta), c.dist * Math.cos(c.phi), c.dist * Math.sin(c.phi) * Math.cos(c.theta)];
                        this.eye = V3.add(c.target, off);
                        this.forward = V3.norm(V3.sub(c.target, this.eye));
                        this.right = V3.norm(V3.cross(this.forward, [0, 1, 0]));
                        this.up = V3.cross(this.right, this.forward);
                        this.vp = matMul(perspective(this.fov, this.w / this.h, .03, 500), lookAt(this.eye, c.target));
                    }
                    project(p) {
                        let m = this.vp
                          , x = p[0]
                          , y = p[1]
                          , z = p[2]
                          , w = m[3] * x + m[7] * y + m[11] * z + m[15];
                        if (w <= 0)
                            return null;
                        let sx = (m[0] * x + m[4] * y + m[8] * z + m[12]) / w
                          , sy = (m[1] * x + m[5] * y + m[9] * z + m[13]) / w;
                        return [(sx + 1) * this.w * .5, (1 - sy) * this.h * .5, w];
                    }
                    pick(x, y) {
                        if (!this.boxes || !this.eye)
                            return null;
                        let yy = (1 - 2 * y / this.h) * Math.tan(this.fov / 2)
                          , xx = (2 * x / this.w - 1) * this.w / this.h * Math.tan(this.fov / 2);
                        const d = V3.norm(V3.add(this.forward, V3.add(V3.scale(this.right, xx), V3.scale(this.up, yy))));
                        let best = Infinity
                          , hit = null;
                        for (const b of this.boxes) {
                            if (!b.meta || b.meta.noPick)
                                continue;
                            let mn = 0
                              , mx = Infinity;
                            for (let k = 0; k < 3; k++) {
                                let half = b.size[k] / 2;
                                if (Math.abs(d[k]) < 1e-9) {
                                    if (this.eye[k] < b.pos[k] - half || this.eye[k] > b.pos[k] + half) {
                                        mx = -1;
                                        break;
                                    }
                                    continue;
                                }
                                let a = (b.pos[k] - half - this.eye[k]) / d[k]
                                  , v = (b.pos[k] + half - this.eye[k]) / d[k];
                                mn = Math.max(mn, Math.min(a, v));
                                mx = Math.min(mx, Math.max(a, v));
                            }
                            if (mx >= mn && mn < best) {
                                best = mn;
                                hit = b;
                            }
                        }
                        let close = null
                          , near = 8;
                        for (const b of this.boxes) {
                            if (!b.meta || !b.meta.weight || b.meta.module !== this.state.selected)
                                continue;
                            const p = this.project(b.pos);
                            if (!p)
                                continue;
                            const d = Math.hypot(p[0] - x, p[1] - y);
                            if (d < near) {
                                near = d;
                                close = b;
                            }
                        }
                        return close || hit;
                    }
                    emit(id, pos, byteCount, color, meta, opt={}) {
                        const targetSide = Math.cbrt(Math.max(0, byteCount) / VOLUME_UNIT)
                          , sx = opt.sx || targetSide
                          , sz = opt.sz || targetSide;
                        let rec = this.items.get(id);
                        if (!rec || rec.lastUsed < this.frameNo - 1) {
                            let from = opt.from;
                            rec = {
                                pos: from ? [...from.pos] : [...pos],
                                bytes: byteCount,
                                sx: from ? from.sx : sx,
                                sz: from ? from.sz : sz
                            };
                            this.items.set(id, rec);
                        }
                        rec.lastUsed = this.frameNo;
                        const a = this.reduced ? 1 : this.damp;
                        rec.pos = rec.pos.map( (v, i) => lerp(v, pos[i], a));
                        rec.bytes = lerp(rec.bytes, byteCount, a);
                        rec.sx = lerp(rec.sx, sx, a);
                        rec.sz = lerp(rec.sz, sz, a);
                        let sy = rec.bytes / VOLUME_UNIT / (rec.sx * rec.sz);
                        const dim = opt.dim || 1
                          , co = (typeof color === 'string' ? rgb(color) : color).map(v => v * dim);
                        const b = {
                            id,
                            pos: [...rec.pos],
                            size: [rec.sx, sy, rec.sz],
                            color: co,
                            glow: opt.glow || 0,
                            style: opt.style || 0,
                            meta,
                            bytes: rec.bytes
                        };
                        if (this.hover && this.hover.id === id)
                            b.glow += .3;
                        this.boxes.push(b);
                        return b;
                    }
                    line(a, b, color, alpha=.4) {
                        const c = typeof color === 'string' ? rgb(color) : color;
                        this.lines.push(...a, ...c, alpha, ...b, ...c, alpha);
                    }
                    point(p, color, size=8, alpha=1) {
                        let c = typeof color === 'string' ? rgb(color) : color;
                        this.points.push(...p, ...c, alpha, size);
                    }
                    curve(a, d, color, alpha=.4, phase=0, bend=1.6, particles=1) {
                        let b = [a[0] + bend, a[1], a[2] + .3]
                          , c = [d[0] - bend, d[1], d[2] + .3]
                          , prev = a;
                        for (let k = 1; k <= 48; k++) {
                            let p = cubic(a, b, c, d, k / 48);
                            this.line(prev, p, color, alpha);
                            prev = p;
                        }
                        for (let n = 0; n < particles; n++) {
                            let t = ((this.clock * .19 + phase + n / particles) % 1 + 1) % 1;
                            this.point(cubic(a, b, c, d, t), color, 9, alpha * 2);
                        }
                    }
                    label(pos, title, sub='', color=COL.muted, priority=0) {
                        this.labels.push({
                            pos,
                            title,
                            sub,
                            color,
                            priority
                        });
                    }
                    layerHeat(i) {
                        let p = phaseAt(this.time, this.state.activity)
                          , t = (this.time - p.from) / (p.to - p.from);
                        if (this.state.activity === 'training') {
                            if (p.name === 'Forward')
                                return Math.exp(-Math.pow((i / 40 - t) * 7, 2)) * .8;
                            if (p.name === 'Backward')
                                return Math.exp(-Math.pow((i / 40 - (1 - t)) * 7, 2)) * .8;
                            if (p.name === 'Update')
                                return .25 + .18 * Math.sin(t * 6.28 + i * .18);
                            return i > 36 ? .35 : .05;
                        }
                        if (p.name === 'Prefill')
                            return i < 20 ? Math.exp(-Math.pow((i / 20 - t) * 7, 2)) * .7 : 0;
                        if (p.name === 'SWA replay')
                            return i >= 20 ? .22 + .12 * Math.sin(i * .5 + this.clock * 4) : .03;
                        if (p.name === 'Decode')
                            return Math.exp(-Math.pow((i / 40 - (t * 2 % 1)) * 8, 2)) * .7;
                        return i >= 37 ? .3 : 0;
                    }
                    tensorLayout(m, mode) {
                        const state = this.state
                          , fs = state.fontScale || 1
                          , key = [m.id, mode, state.detailLayout, state.expertView, fs, Math.round(this.w), Math.round(this.h)].join('|');
                        this.layoutCache = this.layoutCache || new Map;
                        if (this.layoutCache.has(key))
                            return this.layoutCache.get(key);
                        const ws = orderWeights(m)
                          , layer = !!m.mode || m.id.startsWith('D');
                        const entries = ws.map( (w, index) => {
                            const vol = wBytes(w, mode) / VOLUME_UNIT
                              , a = w.shape.length > 1 ? clamp(Math.sqrt(w.shape[w.shape.length - 1] / w.shape[0]), .65, 1.7) : 1
                              , side = Math.cbrt(vol)
                              , sx = side * a * 1.15
                              , sz = side / a
                              , sy = vol / (sx * sz);
                            return {
                                w,
                                index,
                                sx,
                                sy,
                                sz,
                                lane: layer ? (isAttentionTensor(w) ? 0 : 1) : index % 2,
                                rel: [0, 0, 0]
                            };
                        }
                        );
                        let height = 0
                          , width = 10
                          , headings = [];
                        if (state.detailLayout === 'floor') {
                            const cols = layer ? (this.w < 600 ? 2 : 4) : Math.min(3, entries.length)
                              , rows = Math.ceil(entries.length / cols)
                              , cellZ = Math.max(2.8, ...entries.map(e => e.sz + .8))
                              , cellX = Math.max(3.6, ...entries.map(e => e.sx + .8), cellZ * (rows / cols) * (this.w / this.h) * 1.26);
                            entries.forEach( (e, i) => {
                                e.rel = [(i % cols - (cols - 1) / 2) * cellX, e.sy / 2, (Math.floor(i / cols) - (rows - 1) / 2) * cellZ];
                            }
                            );
                            height = rows * cellZ;
                            width = cols * cellX;
                            headings = [{
                                pos: [0, 1, -rows * cellZ / 2 - 1],
                                text: 'INDIVIDUAL STORED TENSORS',
                                sub: 'Each solid is a named tensor or an explicitly counted bank.'
                            }];
                        } else {
                            const laneGap = layer ? 7.4 : Math.max(6, ...entries.map(e => e.sx + e.sz + 1));
                            for (let lane = 0; lane < 2; lane++) {
                                let list = entries.filter(e => e.lane === lane)
                                  , total = list.reduce( (a, e) => a + Math.max(e.sy + .24, .59 * fs), 0);
                                height = Math.max(height, total);
                                let y = total / 2;
                                for (const e of list) {
                                    const step = Math.max(e.sy + .24, .59 * fs);
                                    e.rel = [(lane - .5) * laneGap, y - step / 2, 2.8];
                                    y -= step;
                                }
                            }
                            for (let lane = 0; lane < 2; lane++) {
                                const list = entries.filter(e => e.lane === lane);
                                if (list.length)
                                    headings.push({
                                        pos: [(lane - .5) * laneGap, height / 2 + .65, 2.8],
                                        text: layer ? (lane === 0 ? '01 / ATTENTION + RESIDUAL' : '02 / EXPERTS + RESIDUAL') : (lane === 0 ? 'STORED WEIGHTS' : 'SUPPORTING TENSORS'),
                                        sub: list.length + ' separately modeled tensors'
                                    });
                            }
                            width = laneGap + 5;
                            height += 1.6;
                        }
                        const grid = state.expertView && layer;
                        if (grid) {
                            const n = m.id.startsWith('D') ? 128 : 384
                              , ex = ws.filter(w => w.cat === 'expert')
                              , expB = sumB(ex, mode) / n
                              , side = Math.cbrt(expB / VOLUME_UNIT)
                              , spacing = side + .105
                              , cols = n === 128 ? 16 : 24
                              , rows = n / cols;
                            let others = entries.filter(e => e.w.cat !== 'expert');
                            let maxY = 0;
                            [0, 1].forEach(lane => {
                                let list = others.filter(e => e.lane === lane)
                                  , total = list.length * .59 * fs;
                                maxY = Math.max(maxY, total);
                                list.forEach( (e, i) => {
                                    e.rel = [lane === 0 ? -7.0 : -4.2, total / 2 - (i + .5) * .59 * fs, 2.8];
                                }
                                );
                            }
                            );
                            const center = [3.4, 0, 2.8];
                            height = Math.max(maxY + 2, rows * spacing + 4.8);
                            width = 22;
                            headings = [{
                                pos: [-5.6, maxY / 2 + .8, 2.8],
                                text: 'LAYER-LOCAL TENSORS',
                                sub: 'Hover or click a marker for the exact weight.'
                            }, {
                                pos: [center[0], rows * spacing / 2 + .65, 2.8],
                                text: n + ' EXPERTS / TOP-' + (n === 128 ? 3 : 6),
                                sub: 'Each cube holds gate, up and down. Click to split one.'
                            }];
                            const plan = {
                                entries,
                                height,
                                width,
                                headings,
                                grid: {
                                    n,
                                    ex,
                                    side,
                                    spacing,
                                    cols,
                                    rows,
                                    center,
                                    expB
                                },
                                layer
                            };
                            this.layoutCache.set(key, plan);
                            return plan;
                        }
                        const plan = {
                            entries,
                            height,
                            width,
                            headings,
                            grid: null,
                            layer
                        };
                        this.layoutCache.set(key, plan);
                        return plan;
                    }
                    stackKey(m) {
                        if (m.id === 'embed')
                            return {
                                chain: 0,
                                index: -1
                            };
                        if (m.mode)
                            return {
                                chain: m.index < 20 ? 0 : 1,
                                index: m.index
                            };
                        if (m.id === 'head')
                            return {
                                chain: 1,
                                index: 40
                            };
                        if (m.id.startsWith('D'))
                            return {
                                chain: 1,
                                index: m.index + 1
                            };
                        return null;
                    }
                    viewPos(m) {
                        let p = this.modulePos(m)
                          , mk = this.stackKey(m);
                        for (const [id,e] of this.expands) {
                            if (e < .002 || m.id === id)
                                continue;
                            const other = MODULES.find(x => x.id === id);
                            if (!other)
                                continue;
                            const ok = this.stackKey(other)
                              , plan = this.tensorLayout(other, this.state.precision);
                            if (mk && ok) {
                                const together = mk.chain === ok.chain ? 1 : 1 - this.deck;
                                p[1] += (mk.index < ok.index ? -1 : 1) * (plan.height / 2 + 1.25) * e * together;
                                if (mk.chain !== ok.chain)
                                    p[0] += (mk.chain === 0 ? -1 : 1) * 4.5 * e * this.deck;
                            }
                            if (m.id.startsWith('E') && ok) {
                                p[0] -= 3.5 * e;
                                p[2] -= 2.5 * e;
                            }
                        }
                        return p;
                    }
                    architecture() {
                        const s = this.state
                          , mode = s.precision
                          , selection = s.selected;
                        for (const m of MODULES) {
                            let x = this.expands.get(m.id) || 0
                              , d = selection === m.id ? 1 : 0;
                            x = this.reduced ? d : lerp(x, d, this.damp * .65);
                            if (Math.abs(x - d) < .0008)
                                x = d;
                            this.expands.set(m.id, x);
                        }
                        this.framePositions = new Map;
                        for (const m of MODULES) {
                            const p = this.viewPos(m);
                            this.framePositions.set(m.id, p);
                            const b = sumB(m.ws, mode)
                              , col = m.part === 'encoder' ? COL.enc : m.part === 'decoder' ? COL.dec : m.id[0] === 'E' ? TC.engram : m.id[0] === 'D' ? TC.mhc : m.id === 'vision' || m.id === 'aligner' ? TC.vision : COL.head;
                            const isLayer = !!m.mode || m.id[0] === 'D'
                              , x = this.expands.get(m.id) || 0;
                            if (x > .0001) {
                                this.detailed(m, p, x, mode);
                                continue;
                            }
                            let dim = selection ? .20 : 1;
                            const ph = phaseAt(this.time, this.state.activity).name;
                            if (!selection && ph === 'Prefill' && (m.part === 'decoder' || m.id === 'head' || m.id[0] === 'D'))
                                dim = .32;
                            const f = Math.sqrt(b / sumB(m.ws, 'native'));
                            let opt = isLayer ? {
                                sx: 4 * f,
                                sz: 3.5 * f,
                                glow: m.index < 40 ? this.layerHeat(m.index) : ph === 'DSpark' ? .42 : 0,
                                dim
                            } : m.id[0] === 'E' ? {
                                glow: .02,
                                style: 2,
                                dim
                            } : ['embed', 'head'].includes(m.id) ? {
                                sx: 4 * f,
                                sz: 3.5 * f,
                                dim
                            } : m.id === 'vision' ? {
                                sx: 1.25,
                                sz: .8,
                                dim,
                                style: 3
                            } : m.id === 'aligner' ? {
                                sx: .65,
                                sz: .65,
                                dim
                            } : {
                                dim
                            };
                            const ob = this.emit(m.id, p, b, col, {
                                module: m.id,
                                label: m.label,
                                bytes: b
                            }, opt);
                            if (isLayer) {
                                const c = COL[m.mode] || TC.mhc;
                                this.line([ob.pos[0] - ob.size[0] / 2, ob.pos[1] + ob.size[1] / 2, ob.pos[2] + ob.size[2] / 2], [ob.pos[0] + ob.size[0] / 2, ob.pos[1] + ob.size[1] / 2, ob.pos[2] + ob.size[2] / 2], c, selection ? .17 : .8);
                                if (!selection && (s.labels || m.id[0] === 'D'))
                                    this.label([ob.pos[0] + ob.size[0] / 2 + .35, ob.pos[1], ob.pos[2]], m.id[0] === 'D' ? 'D' + (m.index - 39) : String(m.index).padStart(2, '0'), s.labels ? m.mode || 'draft' : '', c, 1);
                            } else if (!selection) {
                                if (m.id[0] === 'E')
                                    this.label([p[0] - ob.size[0] / 2 - 1.3, p[1], p[2] + 1], m.label, bytes(b, s.binary), col, 6);
                                else
                                    this.label([p[0], p[1] + ob.size[1] / 2 + .5, p[2]], m.label, bytes(b, s.binary), col, 2);
                            }
                        }
                        if (selection)
                            return;
                        const pos = id => this.framePositions.get(id)
                          , split = this.deck > .85;
                        if (split) {
                            this.label(V3.add(pos('L19'), [-.4, 1.5, 2]), 'ENCODER / L00-L19', 'First 20 layers / runs in decode', COL.enc, 8);
                            this.label(V3.add(pos('L36'), [4.2, 0, 1]), 'DECODER / L20-L39', 'Next 20 layers / runs in decode', COL.dec, 8);
                        } else {
                            this.label(V3.add(pos('D2'), [0, 1, 0]), 'ONE CHECKPOINT / L00-L39', 'Ordered layers, not physical shard offsets.', COL.muted, 9);
                        }
                        const boundary = V3.add(pos('L20'), [-3.2, 0, 1.3]);
                        this.label(boundary, 'L19 -> L20', phaseAt(this.time, s.activity).name === 'Prefill' ? 'Optimized prefill: prepare decoder KV' : 'Forward pass continues. No model swap.', COL.full, 7);
                        // Each layer gets a small address label; the side rail remains the accessible list.
                        if (!this.state.labels)
                            for (const m of LAYERS) {
                                const p = pos(m.id);
                                this.label(V3.add(p, [2.5, 0, 1.9]), 'L' + String(m.index).padStart(2, '0'), '', m.index < 20 ? COL.enc : COL.dec, 1);
                            }
                        if (!s.links)
                            return;
                        this.curve(pos('vision'), pos('aligner'), TC.vision, .5, .4, .4);
                        this.curve(pos('aligner'), pos('embed'), TC.vision, .5, .2, .6);
                        for (const e of ENGRAM) {
                            const p = pos(e.id)
                              , lp = pos('L' + e.index);
                            this.curve(V3.add(p, [2.3, 0, 0]), V3.add(lp, [-2, 0, 0]), TC.engram, .4, e.index * .03, 2, 2);
                        }
                        this.curve(V3.add(pos('L19'), [-2, .3, 0]), V3.add(pos('L20'), [-2, 0, 0]), COL.full, .42, .3, split ? 7 : 3, 2);
                        if (split) {
                            const a = V3.add(pos('L19'), [0, 0, 2.15])
                              , b = V3.add(pos('L20'), [0, 0, 2.15])
                              , v = V3.norm(V3.sub(b, a))
                              , side = V3.norm(V3.cross(v, [0, 0, 1]));
                            this.line(a, b, COL.dec, .55);
                            const tip = a.map( (x, i) => lerp(x, b[i], .56));
                            this.line(tip, tip.map( (x, i) => x - v[i] * .65 + side[i] * .28), COL.dec, .8);
                            this.line(tip, tip.map( (x, i) => x - v[i] * .65 - side[i] * .28), COL.dec, .8);
                        }
                        CT.kv_source_layer_ids.forEach( (id, k) => {
                            const p = pos('L' + id)
                              , cp = [p[0] + 3.8, p[1] + .2, -2.6]
                              , n = id < 20 ? Math.floor(s.context / 2) : s.context
                              , kv = n * 356;
                            this.emit('cache:' + id, cp, kv, COL.full, {
                                module: 'L' + id,
                                label: 'L' + id + ' global KV activations',
                                bytes: kv,
                                noPick: true
                            }, {
                                glow: .14
                            });
                            this.curve([p[0] + 2, p[1], 0], cp, COL.full, .24, k * .15, .65);
                        }
                        );
                        const ph = phaseAt(this.time, s.activity)
                          , progress = (this.time - ph.from) / (ph.to - ph.from)
                          , train = s.activity === 'training';
                        let ids = ph.name === 'Prefill' ? Array.from({
                            length: 20
                        }, (_, i) => 'L' + i) : ph.name === 'SWA replay' ? Array.from({
                            length: 20
                        }, (_, i) => 'L' + (20 + i)) : ['embed', ...LAYERS.map(m => m.id), 'head'];
                        if (train && ph.name === 'Backward')
                            ids = ids.slice().reverse();
                        if (['Prefill', 'SWA replay', 'Decode', 'Forward', 'Backward'].includes(ph.name)) {
                            for (let lane = 0; lane < 4; lane++) {
                                const offset = [-.7 + lane * .46, 0, 2.15];
                                for (let i = 1; i < ids.length; i++)
                                    this.line(V3.add(pos(ids[i - 1]), offset), V3.add(pos(ids[i]), offset), ph.color, .16);
                                const frac = train ? clamp(progress + lane * .009, 0, .999) : ((progress * (ph.name === 'Decode' ? 2 : 1) + lane * .045) % 1)
                                  , at = frac * (ids.length - 1)
                                  , lo = Math.floor(at)
                                  , hi = Math.min(lo + 1, ids.length - 1)
                                  , a = pos(ids[lo])
                                  , b = pos(ids[hi]);
                                this.point(a.map( (v, k) => lerp(v, b[k], at - lo) + offset[k]), ph.color, 9, .9);
                            }
                        }
                        if (train) {
                            const hp = pos('head');
                            if (ph.name === 'Loss')
                                this.label(V3.add(hp, [4, .6, 0]), 'PREDICTION vs TARGET', 'Loss (schematic)', COL.engram, 10);
                            if (ph.name === 'Backward')
                                this.label(V3.add(pos('L20'), [4, 0, 0]), 'GRADIENTS', 'Backward path, not reverse inference.', COL.mhc, 10);
                            if (ph.name === 'Update')
                                this.label(V3.add(pos('L20'), [4, 0, 0]), 'UPDATE VALUES', 'Shapes and modeled bytes stay fixed.', COL.full, 10);
                        }
                        if (!train && ph.name === 'DSpark')
                            for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++)
                                this.curve(pos('L' + (37 + i)), pos('D' + i), TC.mhc, .55, i * .3, 3, 2);
                    }

                    detailed(m, p, t, mode) {
                        const plan = this.tensorLayout(m, mode)
                          , ws = plan.entries
                          , total = sumB(m.ws, mode)
                          , f = Math.sqrt(total / sumB(m.ws, 'native'))
                          , layer = plan.layer;
                        let sx0 = layer || ['embed', 'head'].includes(m.id) ? 4 * f : m.id === 'vision' ? 1.25 : m.id === 'aligner' ? .65 : Math.cbrt(total / VOLUME_UNIT)
                          , sz0 = layer || ['embed', 'head'].includes(m.id) ? 3.5 * f : m.id === 'vision' ? .8 : m.id === 'aligner' ? .65 : sx0;
                        const height0 = total / VOLUME_UNIT / (sx0 * sz0);
                        let startY = p[1] - height0 / 2;
                        const selected = m.id === this.state.selected
                          , focusName = this.state.hoverTensor || this.state.tensor;
                        for (const e of ws) {
                            const w = e.w;
                            if (plan.grid && w.cat === 'expert')
                                continue;
                            const b = wBytes(w, mode)
                              , sliceH = b / VOLUME_UNIT / (sx0 * sz0)
                              , from = {
                                pos: [p[0], startY + sliceH / 2, p[2]],
                                sx: sx0,
                                sz: sz0
                            };
                            startY += sliceH;
                            const local = clamp(t * 1.16 - e.index / ws.length * .16, 0, 1)
                              , a = smooth(local)
                              , dest = V3.add(p, e.rel)
                              , pos = from.pos.map( (v, i) => lerp(v, dest[i], a))
                              , sx = Math.exp(lerp(Math.log(sx0), Math.log(e.sx), a))
                              , sz = Math.exp(lerp(Math.log(sz0), Math.log(e.sz), a));
                            const hot = selected && focusName === w.name
                              , ob = this.emit(m.id + ':w:' + w.name, pos, b, tensorColor(w), {
                                module: m.id,
                                tensor: w.name,
                                label: tensorInfo(w, m).title,
                                bytes: b,
                                weight: w
                            }, {
                                sx,
                                sz,
                                from,
                                glow: hot ? .75 : w.cat === 'shared' ? .17 : .03,
                                dim: selected ? 1 : .35
                            });
                            const tiny = w.p < 100000;
                            const sc = this.project(ob.pos);
                            if (selected && t > .4 && sc) {
                                const pixels = Math.max(...ob.size) * this.h / (2 * Math.tan(this.fov / 2) * sc[2]);
                                if (pixels < 6)
                                    this.point(ob.pos, tensorColor(w), hot ? 9 : 6, hot ? 1 : .8);
                            }
                            if (selected && t > .57 && (!plan.grid || hot)) {
                                this.detailLabels.push({
                                    m,
                                    w,
                                    ob,
                                    lane: e.lane,
                                    order: e.index,
                                    tiny,
                                    hot,
                                    layout: this.state.detailLayout
                                });
                            }
                        }
                        if (plan.grid) {
                            const g = plan.grid
                              , top = new Set(pickExperts(m.index * 1000 + Math.floor(this.clock * .65), g.n, g.n === 128 ? 3 : 6))
                              , center = V3.add(p, g.center)
                              , exParts = g.ex.map(w => ({
                                w,
                                b: wBytes(w, mode) / g.n
                            }));
                            let expertFocus = this.state.expert;
                            for (let i = 0; i < g.n; i++) {
                                const dest = [center[0] + (i % g.cols - (g.cols - 1) / 2) * g.spacing, center[1] + ((g.rows - 1) / 2 - Math.floor(i / g.cols)) * g.spacing, center[2]]
                                  , a = smooth(t)
                                  , origin = [p[0], startY + (i + .5) * g.expB / VOLUME_UNIT / (sx0 * sz0), p[2]]
                                  , pos = origin.map( (v, k) => lerp(v, dest[k], a));
                                if (selected && expertFocus === i) {
                                    let sum = 0;
                                    for (let j = 0; j < exParts.length; j++) {
                                        let item = exParts[j]
                                          , w = {
                                            ...item.w,
                                            count: 1,
                                            p: item.w.p / g.n
                                        }
                                          , h = item.b / VOLUME_UNIT / (g.side * g.side)
                                          , from = {
                                            pos: [pos[0], pos[1] - g.side / 2 + sum + h / 2, pos[2]],
                                            sx: g.side,
                                            sz: g.side
                                        };
                                        sum += h;
                                        const side = Math.cbrt(item.b / VOLUME_UNIT)
                                          , d = [center[0] + (j - 1) * 1.3, center[1] - g.rows * g.spacing / 2 - 1.4, center[2] + .3]
                                          , ob = this.emit(m.id + ':ex:' + i + ':' + w.name, d, item.b, tensorColor(w), {
                                            module: m.id,
                                            expert: i,
                                            tensor: w.name,
                                            label: 'Expert ' + i + ' / ' + tensorInfo(w, m).title,
                                            bytes: item.b,
                                            weight: w
                                        }, {
                                            sx: side * 1.2,
                                            sz: side / 1.2,
                                            from,
                                            glow: .5
                                        });
                                        if (t > .65)
                                            this.label([ob.pos[0], ob.pos[1] - .42, ob.pos[2]], w.name.includes('.w1.') ? 'W1 / gate' : w.name.includes('.w3.') ? 'W3 / up' : 'W2 / down', bytes(item.b, this.state.binary), tensorColor(w), 8);
                                    }
                                    const ring = g.side * .62;
                                    this.line([dest[0] - ring, dest[1] - ring, dest[2]], [dest[0] + ring, dest[1] - ring, dest[2]], TC.expert, .5);
                                    continue;
                                }
                                const f0 = {
                                    pos: origin,
                                    sx: sx0,
                                    sz: sz0
                                }
                                  , ss = Math.exp(lerp(Math.log(sx0), Math.log(g.side), a))
                                  , zz = Math.exp(lerp(Math.log(sz0), Math.log(g.side), a));
                                const ob = this.emit(m.id + ':ex:' + i, pos, g.expB, top.has(i) ? TC.shared : TC.expert, {
                                    module: m.id,
                                    expert: i,
                                    label: 'Expert ' + String(i).padStart(3, '0'),
                                    bytes: g.expB,
                                    active: top.has(i)
                                }, {
                                    sx: ss,
                                    sz: zz,
                                    from: f0,
                                    glow: top.has(i) ? .65 : 0,
                                    dim: top.has(i) ? 1 : .6
                                });
                                if (selected && t > .85 && top.has(i) && this.state.links) {
                                    const source = V3.add(p, [-4.2, 3.4, 2.8]);
                                    this.line(source, ob.pos, TC.router, .16);
                                    this.point(ob.pos, TC.shared, 6, .8);
                                }
                            }
                        }
                        if (selected && t > .6) {
                            if (this.w >= 600)
                                for (const h of plan.headings)
                                    this.label(V3.add(p, h.pos), h.text, h.sub, plan.grid ? TC.expert : COL.muted, 9);
                            if (this.state.links && !plan.grid && this.state.detailLayout === 'column') {
                                // Only genuine data dependencies are animated. Parameter ordering is not an execution trace.
                                const get = n => this.boxes.find(b => b.id === m.id + ':w:' + n);
                                const pairs = [['attn.wq_a.weight', 'attn.q_norm.weight'], ['attn.q_norm.weight', 'attn.wq_b.weight'], ['attn.wo_a.weight', 'attn.wo_b.weight'], ['ffn.gate.weight', 'ffn.experts.w1.weight'], ['ffn.gate.weight', 'ffn.experts.w3.weight'], ['ffn.experts.w1.weight', 'ffn.experts.w2.weight'], ['ffn.experts.w3.weight', 'ffn.experts.w2.weight']];
                                for (let i = 0; i < pairs.length; i++) {
                                    const a = get(pairs[i][0])
                                      , b = get(pairs[i][1]);
                                    if (a && b) {
                                        let p1 = V3.add(a.pos, [0, 0, a.size[2] / 2 + .12])
                                          , p2 = V3.add(b.pos, [0, 0, b.size[2] / 2 + .12]);
                                        this.line(p1, p2, TC.q, .16);
                                        this.point(p1.map( (v, k) => lerp(v, p2[k], (this.clock * .4 + i * .23) % 1)), TC.q, 6, .7);
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    commitDetailLabels() {
                        this.detailPool = this.detailPool || [];
                        this.detailLines = this.detailLines || [];
                        if (!this.leaderSvg) {
                            this.leaderSvg = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', 'svg');
                            this.leaderSvg.classList.add('tensor-leaders');
                            this.labelRoot.prepend(this.leaderSvg);
                        }
                        const fs = this.state.fontScale || 1
                          , small = this.w < 600
                          , grid = this.state.expertView
                          , floor = this.state.detailLayout === 'floor'
                          , raw = this.detailLabels.filter(l => this.project(l.ob.pos));
                        let queue = [];
                        const tools = document.querySelector('.expansion-tools')
                          , world = this.canvas.getBoundingClientRect()
                          , toolsBottom = tools ? tools.getBoundingClientRect().bottom - world.top : 80;
                        const top = Math.max(100, toolsBottom + 32)
                          , bottom = this.h - 43
                          , available = Math.max(30, bottom - top);
                        if (!floor && !grid) {
                            for (let lane = 0; lane < 2; lane++) {
                                let list = raw.filter(l => l.lane === lane).map(l => ({
                                    ...l,
                                    sc: this.project(l.ob.pos)
                                })).sort( (a, b) => a.sc[1] - b.sc[1]);
                                const compactHeight = Math.ceil((small ? 13 : 15) * fs) + 4
                                  , fullHeight = Math.ceil(28 * fs) + 5;
                                const compact = list.reduce( (a, l) => a + (l.tiny ? compactHeight : fullHeight) + 3, 0) > available;
                                // Do not shrink fonts to squeeze in more labels. Subsample only labels when necessary;
                                // all weight solids and the full inspector inventory remain available for picking.
                                const cap = Math.max(1, Math.floor(available / (compactHeight + 3)));
                                if (compact && list.length > cap) {
                                    let keep = new Set();
                                    list.forEach( (l, i) => {
                                        if (l.hot)
                                            keep.add(i);
                                    }
                                    );
                                    for (let k = 0; keep.size < cap && k < cap; k++)
                                        keep.add(Math.round(k * (list.length - 1) / Math.max(1, cap - 1)));
                                    list = list.filter( (l, i) => keep.has(i));
                                }
                                for (const l of list) {
                                    l.h = compact || l.tiny ? compactHeight : fullHeight;
                                    l.compact = compact || l.tiny;
                                }
                                let previous = top;
                                for (const l of list) {
                                    l.y = Math.max(previous, l.sc[1] - l.h / 2);
                                    previous = l.y + l.h + 3;
                                }
                                if (list.length) {
                                    const excess = Math.max(0, list[list.length - 1].y + list[list.length - 1].h - bottom);
                                    for (let i = list.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
                                        list[i].y -= excess;
                                        if (i < list.length - 1)
                                            list[i].y = Math.min(list[i].y, list[i + 1].y - list[i].h - 3);
                                    }
                                    if (list[0].y < top) {
                                        const add = top - list[0].y;
                                        list.forEach(l => l.y += add);
                                    }
                                }
                                const width = small ? Math.min(150 * fs, this.w * .34) : Math.min(270 * fs, this.w * .27);
                                for (const l of list) {
                                    const px = l.ob.size[0] * this.h / (2 * Math.tan(this.fov / 2) * l.sc[2]);
                                    l.width = width;
                                    l.x = lane === 0 ? l.sc[0] - px / 2 - width - 18 : l.sc[0] + px / 2 + 18;
                                    l.x = clamp(l.x, 8, this.w - width - 8);
                                    queue.push(l);
                                }
                            }
                        } else {
                            const sorted = raw.map(l => ({
                                ...l,
                                sc: this.project(l.ob.pos)
                            })).sort( (a, b) => Number(b.hot) - Number(a.hot) || a.sc[1] - b.sc[1])
                              , placed = [];
                            const cols = small ? 2 : 4
                              , width = floor && !grid ? Math.min(235 * fs, this.w / cols * .82) : Math.min(195 * fs, this.w * (small ? .32 : .22))
                              , h = Math.ceil((small ? 14 : 28) * fs) + 5;
                            for (const l of sorted) {
                                if (l.sc[0] < 0 || l.sc[0] > this.w || l.sc[1] < top - 20 || l.sc[1] > bottom)
                                    continue;
                                let x = clamp(l.sc[0] - width / 2, 7, this.w - width - 7)
                                  , y = l.sc[1] + 10;
                                for (let j = 0; j < 20 && placed.some(r => x < r.x + r.width + 3 && x + width + 3 > r.x && y < r.y + r.h + 3 && y + h + 3 > r.y); j++)
                                    y += h + 3;
                                if (y + h > bottom)
                                    continue;
                                const q = {
                                    ...l,
                                    x,
                                    y,
                                    width,
                                    h,
                                    compact: small
                                };
                                placed.push(q);
                                queue.push(q);
                            }
                        }
                        let count = 0;
                        for (const l of queue) {
                            let node = this.detailPool[count]
                              , line = this.detailLines[count];
                            if (!node) {
                                node = document.createElement('button');
                                node.type = 'button';
                                node.className = 'tensor-label';
                                const a = document.createElement('b')
                                  , b = document.createElement('span');
                                node.append(a, b);
                                this.labelRoot.append(node);
                                node.addEventListener('pointerenter', () => {
                                    this.labelHover = true;
                                    const d = node._datum;
                                    this.cb.hover && this.cb.hover(d.w.name);
                                }
                                );
                                node.addEventListener('pointerleave', () => {
                                    this.labelHover = false;
                                    this.cb.hover && this.cb.hover(null);
                                }
                                );
                                node.addEventListener('click', e => {
                                    e.stopPropagation();
                                    const d = node._datum;
                                    this.cb.select(d.m.id, d.w.name, null);
                                }
                                );
                                this.detailPool.push(node);
                                line = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', 'path');
                                this.leaderSvg.append(line);
                                this.detailLines.push(line);
                            }
                            node._datum = l;
                            node.children[0].textContent = tensorShort(l.w) + (l.w.count > 1 ? ' \u00d7' + l.w.count : '');
                            node.children[1].textContent = bytes(wBytes(l.w, this.state.precision), this.state.binary) + ' / ' + wFormat(l.w, this.state.precision);
                            node.title = tensorInfo(l.w, l.m).title + '\n[' + l.w.shape.join(', ') + ']\nClick to pin the explanation';
                            node.className = 'tensor-label' + (l.compact ? ' compact' : '') + (l.hot ? ' hot' : '');
                            node.style.cssText = 'display:block;left:' + l.x + 'px;top:' + l.y + 'px;width:' + l.width + 'px;height:' + l.h + 'px;--tensor:' + tensorColor(l.w);
                            node.setAttribute('aria-label', l.w.name + ', ' + node.children[1].textContent + ', inspect');
                            const endX = l.sc[0] < l.x ? l.x : l.sc[0] > l.x + l.width ? l.x + l.width : clamp(l.sc[0], l.x, l.x + l.width)
                              , endY = l.y + l.h / 2;
                            line.setAttribute('d', 'M' + l.sc[0] + ',' + l.sc[1] + ' L' + (l.sc[0] + endX) / 2 + ',' + endY + ' L' + endX + ',' + endY);
                            line.setAttribute('stroke', tensorColor(l.w));
                            line.setAttribute('opacity', l.hot ? '.85' : '.32');
                            line.style.display = 'block';
                            count++;
                        }
                        for (let i = count; i < this.detailPool.length; i++) {
                            this.detailPool[i].style.display = 'none';
                            this.detailLines[i].style.display = 'none';
                        }
                    }
                    focusTensor(name) {
                        const ob = this.boxes.find(b => b.meta && b.meta.module === this.state.selected && b.meta.tensor === name);
                        if (!ob)
                            return;
                        this.to.target = [...ob.pos];
                        this.to.dist = Math.max(3.8, Math.max(...ob.size) * 8);
                        this.to.theta = .35;
                        this.to.phi = 1.1;
                    }
                    diagnostics() {
                        const model = new Map;
                        let maxError = 0;
                        for (const b of this.boxes) {
                            const v = b.size.reduce( (a, n) => a * n, 1) * VOLUME_UNIT;
                            maxError = Math.max(maxError, Math.abs(v - b.bytes) / Math.max(1, b.bytes));
                            if (!b.meta || b.id.startsWith('cache:') || b.id.startsWith('kv:'))
                                continue;
                            let id = b.meta.module;
                            if (MODULES.some(m => m.id === id))
                                model.set(id, (model.get(id) || 0) + b.bytes);
                        }
                        return {
                            precision: this.state.precision,
                            selected: this.state.selected,
                            solidCount: this.boxes.length,
                            maxRelativeVolumeError: maxError,
                            modules: [...model].map( ([id,actual]) => ({
                                id,
                                actual,
                                expected: sumB(MODULES.find(m => m.id === id).ws, this.state.precision)
                            })),
                            visibleTensorLabels: (this.detailPool || []).filter(n => n.style.display !== 'none').length
                        };
                    }
                    storage() {
                        let s = this.state
                          , rSide = Math.cbrt(sumB(CATEGORIES[0][3], s.precision) / VOLUME_UNIT)
                          , eSide = Math.cbrt(sumB(CATEGORIES[1][3], s.precision) / VOLUME_UNIT)
                          , coords = [[-2.6 - rSide / 2, 0, 0], [-1.4 + eSide / 2, 0, 0], [-1.4 + eSide + 2.4, 0, -2.1], [-1.4 + eSide + 5.4, 0, -2.1], [-1.4 + eSide + 5.4, 0, 2], [-1.4 + eSide + 2.4, 0, 2], [-4, 0, Math.max(rSide, eSide) / 2 + 2.4], [.6, 0, Math.max(rSide, eSide) / 2 + 2.4]];
                        for (let i = 0; i < CATEGORIES.length; i++) {
                            let[id,name,col,ws] = CATEGORIES[i]
                              , b = sumB(ws, s.precision)
                              , side = Math.cbrt(b / VOLUME_UNIT)
                              , p = [coords[i][0], side / 2, coords[i][2]];
                            let ob = this.emit('category:' + id, p, b, col, {
                                module: 'cat:' + id,
                                label: name,
                                bytes: b
                            }, {
                                style: id === 'engram' ? 2 : 0,
                                glow: id === 'routed' ? .06 : 0
                            });
                            this.label([p[0], ob.size[1] + .6, p[2]], name, bytes(b, s.binary) + ' / ' + (b / TOTALS[s.precision] * 100).toFixed(1) + '%', col, 3);
                        }
                        for (let i = -16; i <= 14; i += 2) {
                            this.line([i, -.02, -6], [i, -.02, 9], '#38516a', .14);
                            this.line([-16, -.02, i / 2], [14, -.02, i / 2], '#38516a', .14);
                        }
                        this.label([-2, Math.max(10, rSide + 1.5), -3], 'THE CHECKPOINT, BY VOLUME', 'One cubic world unit = one decimal GB. No category is enlarged.', COL.muted, 8);
                    }
                    cache() {
                        const s = this.state
                          , ids = [2, 8, 14, 20];
                        ids.forEach( (id, i) => {
                            let n = id < 20 ? Math.floor(s.context / 2) : s.context
                              , b = n * 356
                              , p = [(i - 1.5) * 1.45, 1.55, 0]
                              , side = Math.cbrt(b / VOLUME_UNIT);
                            this.emit('kv:' + id, p, b, i === 3 ? COL.dec : COL.full, {
                                module: 'L' + id,
                                label: 'L' + id + ' global KV + index keys',
                                bytes: b
                            }, {
                                glow: .12
                            });
                            this.label([p[0], 2.0 + side / 2, 0], 'L' + String(id).padStart(2, '0'), (id < 20 ? '2:1 / ' : '1:1 / ') + bytes(b, s.binary), i === 3 ? COL.dec : COL.full, 5);
                            if (s.links)
                                this.curve([p[0], 1.2, 0], [0, -.2, 0], i === 3 ? COL.dec : COL.full, .3, i * .18, .6, 2);
                        }
                        );
                        const selected = pickExperts(Math.floor(this.clock * .4), 2048, 512)
                          , set = new Set(selected);
                        for (let i = 0; i < 2048; i++) {
                            let x = (i % 64 - 31.5) * .077
                              , y = -.5 - Math.floor(i / 64) * .052;
                            this.point([x, y, 0], set.has(i) ? COL.dec : '#4a6280', set.has(i) ? 3.3 : 2, set.has(i) ? .72 : .22);
                        }
                        this.label([0, -2.53, 0], '2,048 CANDIDATE BLOCKS x 8', 'A schematic pool; the true cap is 16,384 candidate positions.', COL.muted, 7);
                        this.label([0, -3.23, 0], 'TOP-512 POSITIONS PER QUERY', '512 illuminated sample marks illustrate sparse selection.', COL.dec, 6);
                        this.label([0, 3.25, 0], 'FOUR SHARED GLOBAL BANKS', '890 B / original token across the model, not per layer.', COL.full, 8);
                    }
                    commitLabels() {
                        let placed = []
                          , use = 0;
                        const q = this.labels.map(l => ({
                            ...l,
                            screen: this.project(l.pos)
                        })).filter(l => l.screen && l.screen[0] > -60 && l.screen[0] < this.w + 60 && l.screen[1] > 15 && l.screen[1] < this.h - 30).sort( (a, b) => b.priority - a.priority);
                        for (const l of q) {
                            let[x,y] = l.screen;
                            const fs = this.state.fontScale || 1;
                            let width = Math.min(420, Math.max(l.title.length * 6.7, l.sub.length * 5.25) * fs + 16)
                              , h = (l.sub ? 33 : 18) * fs;
                            if (x - width / 2 < 8 || x + width / 2 > this.w - 8)
                                continue;
                            let rect = {
                                x: x - width / 2,
                                y: y - h / 2,
                                w: width,
                                h
                            };
                            if (placed.some(r => rect.x < r.x + r.w + 7 && rect.x + rect.w + 7 > r.x && rect.y < r.y + r.h + 4 && rect.y + rect.h + 4 > r.y))
                                continue;
                            if (this.state.view === 'architecture' && y < 95 && x < 430)
                                continue;
                            placed.push(rect);
                            let node = this.labelPool[use++];
                            if (!node) {
                                node = document.createElement('div');
                                node.className = 'world-label';
                                let a = document.createElement('b')
                                  , b = document.createElement('span');
                                node.append(a, b);
                                this.labelRoot.append(node);
                                this.labelPool.push(node);
                            }
                            if (node.children[0].textContent !== l.title)
                                node.children[0].textContent = l.title;
                            if (node.children[1].textContent !== l.sub)
                                node.children[1].textContent = l.sub;
                            node.children[0].style.color = l.color;
                            node.style.transform = `translate(${x}px,${y}px) translate(-50%,-50%)`;
                            node.style.display = 'block';
                        }
                        for (let i = use; i < this.labelPool.length; i++)
                            this.labelPool[i].style.display = 'none';
                    }
                    updateTooltip() {
                        if (this.labelHover) {
                            this.tip.style.display = 'none';
                            return;
                        }
                        const h = !this.drag && this.mx >= 0 ? this.pick(this.mx, this.my) : null;
                        this.hover = h;
                        const name = h && h.meta.weight ? h.meta.tensor : null;
                        if (name !== this.lastHoverName) {
                            this.lastHoverName = name;
                            this.cb.hover && this.cb.hover(name);
                        }
                        if (!h) {
                            this.tip.style.display = 'none';
                            this.canvas.style.cursor = this.drag ? 'grabbing' : 'grab';
                            return;
                        }
                        this.canvas.style.cursor = 'pointer';
                        const m = h.meta
                          , mod = MODULES.find(x => x.id === m.module)
                          , info = m.weight ? tensorInfo(m.weight, mod) : mod ? moduleExplanation(mod) : null;
                        this.tip.style.display = 'block';
                        this.tip.children[0].textContent = m.weight ? info.title : m.label;
                        this.tip.children[0].style.color = m.weight ? tensorColor(m.weight) : '#d6e8ff';
                        this.tip.children[1].textContent = (m.weight ? '[' + m.weight.shape.map(num).join(', ') + '] / ' : '') + bytes(m.bytes, this.state.binary) + (m.expert !== undefined ? ' / expert ' + m.expert : ' / click to pin');
                        this.tip.children[2].textContent = m.weight ? info.job : m.expert !== undefined ? 'One specialist holds three matrices: gate, up and down. Click to separate them. Routing is illustrative; light changes, volume does not.' : info ? info.body : '';
                        const width = Math.min(330, this.w - 16)
                          , height = this.tip.offsetHeight || 170;
                        this.tip.style.width = width + 'px';
                        this.tip.style.left = clamp(this.mx + 17, 8, this.w - width - 8) + 'px';
                        this.tip.style.top = clamp(this.my + 18, 8, this.h - height - 8) + 'px';
                    }
                    loop(now) {
                        if (document.hidden) {
                            this.last = now;
                            this.raf = requestAnimationFrame(this.loop);
                            return;
                        }
                        if (this.w < 600 && this.lastRendered && now - this.lastRendered < 32) {
                            this.raf = requestAnimationFrame(this.loop);
                            return;
                        }
                        this.lastRendered = now;
                        this.frameNo++;
                        let dt = Math.min(.05, Math.max(.001, (now - this.last) / 1000));
                        this.last = now;
                        this.damp = 1 - Math.exp(-dt * 10);
                        if (this.state.playing && !document.hidden) {
                            this.clock += dt * this.state.speed;
                            const phase = phaseAt(this.time, this.state.activity)
                              , next = this.time + dt * this.state.speed;
                            this.time = this.state.loopPhase ? phase.from + (next - phase.from) % (phase.to - phase.from) : next % 22;
                        }
                        const targetDeck = this.state.layout === 'split' ? 1 : 0;
