                        this.deck = this.reduced ? targetDeck : clamp(this.deck + Math.sign(targetDeck - this.deck) * Math.min(Math.abs(targetDeck - this.deck), dt / 1.35), 0, 1);
                        this.camera(dt);
                        this.boxes = [];
                        this.lines = [];
                        this.points = [];
                        this.labels = [];
                        this.detailLabels = [];
                        if (this.state.view === 'storage')
                            this.storage();
                        else if (this.state.view === 'cache')
                            this.cache();
                        else
                            this.architecture();
                        this.updateTooltip();
                        this.renderer.render(this.boxes, this.lines, this.points, this.vp, this.eye);
                        this.commitLabels();
                        this.commitDetailLabels();
                        if (now - this.lastTick > 140) {
                            this.cb.tick(this.time);
                            this.lastTick = now;
                        }
                        this.raf = requestAnimationFrame(this.loop);
                    }
                    snapshot() {
                        return this.canvas.toDataURL('image/png');
                    }
                    dispose() {
                        cancelAnimationFrame(this.raf);
                        this.ro.disconnect();
                        this.disposers.forEach(f => f());
                        this.renderer.dispose();
                        this.labelRoot.replaceChildren();
                    }
                }

                // ---------- app.js ----------
                const {h, render, Component, createRef} = Preact;
                const html = htm.bind(h);
                const ICONS = {
                    play: 'M8 5L19 12L8 19Z',
                    pause: 'M8 5V19M16 5V19',
                    reset: 'M3 10A9 9 0 1 1 5 19M3 4V10H9',
                    expand: 'M4 9V4H9M15 4H20V9M20 15V20H15M9 20H4V15',
                    close: 'M6 6L18 18M18 6L6 18',
                    help: 'M9 8A3 3 0 1 1 14 10C12 11 12 12 12 14M12 17V18',
                    link: 'M9 15L15 9M7 14L5 16A3 3 0 0 0 9 20L12 17M12 7L15 4A3 3 0 0 1 19 8L17 10',
                    labels: 'M4 5H20M12 5V20M8 20H16',
                    download: 'M12 3V16M7 11L12 16L17 11M4 17V21H20V17',
                    camera: 'M4 7H8L10 4H15L17 7H21V20H3V7ZM16 13A4 4 0 1 1 8 13A4 4 0 1 1 16 13',
                    arrow: 'M6 12H18M13 7L18 12L13 17',
                    chevron: 'M8 5L15 12L8 19',
                    menu: 'M4 6H20M4 12H20M4 18H20',
                    up: 'M6 15L12 9L18 15',
                    down: 'M6 9L12 15L18 9'
                };
                function Icon({name, size=18}) {
                    return html`<svg width=${size} height=${size} viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="1.6" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round" aria-hidden="true"><path d=${ICONS[name] || ICONS.help}/></svg>`;
                }
                function Section({title, children, tag}) {
                    return html`<section class="section"><div class="section-head"><h3>${title}</h3>${tag && html`<span>${tag}</span>`}</div>${children}</section>`;
                }
                function Fact({label, value, color}) {
                    return html`<div class="fact"><span>${label}</span><b style=${color ? {
                        color
                    } : null}>${value}</b></div>`;
                }
                function Note({children, color=COL.enc}) {
                    return html`<div class="note" style=${{
                        '--note': color
                    }}>${children}</div>`;
                }
                function Chip({children, color=COL.enc}) {
                    return html`<span class="chip" style=${{
                        color,
                        borderColor: color + '55',
                        background: color + '0c'
                    }}>${children}</span>`;
                }
                function safeDownload(name, content, type='application/json') {
                    const blob = content instanceof Blob ? content : new Blob([content],{
                        type
                    })
                      , url = URL.createObjectURL(blob)
                      , a = document.createElement('a');
                    a.href = url;
                    a.download = name;
                    a.click();
                    setTimeout( () => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 2000);
                }
                function moduleExplanation(m) {
                    if (!m)
                        return EXP.overview;
                    if (m.mode)
                        return EXP[m.mode];
                    if (m.id[0] === 'E')
                        return EXP.engram;
                    if (m.id[0] === 'D')
                        return EXP.dspark;
                    if (m.id === 'vision' || m.id === 'aligner')
                        return EXP.vision;
                    return m.id === 'head' ? {
                        title: 'The untied output vocabulary.',
                        body: 'The 5,120-dimensional representation projects to 129,280 logits. The output weights are separate from the input embedding. Disk storage is BF16 in this estimate; reference-runtime F32 expansion is deliberately not charged to the checkpoint.'
                    } : {
                        title: 'A shared text-and-image stream.',
                        body: 'A 129,280 by 5,120 BF16 table supplies text embeddings. The vision projector supplies vectors of the same width. Each token travels through four mHC residual streams; those streams do not multiply the stored embedding weights.'
                    };
                }
                function describeFormat(w) {
                    return w.note || ({
                        fp4: 'Packed E2M1, two logical values per byte, with one E8M0 scale for every 32 values in the native layout.',
                        fp8: 'FP8 weights with one one-byte E8M0 scale per 32 by 32 weight block.',
                        fp8row: 'FP8 data with one one-byte scale per group of 32 values in each row.',
                        bf16: 'Two bytes per logical value in the native storage model.',
                        f32: 'Four bytes per logical value in the native storage model.'
                    }[w.format] || 'See the embedded configuration and reference implementation.');
                }
                function resolveAuditModule(name) {
                    let n = name.replace(/^(?:model\.|language_model\.|text_model\.)+/, '');
                    let a = n.match(/^layers\.(\d+)\.engram\./);
                    if (a)
                        return 'E' + a[1];
                    a = n.match(/^layers\.(\d+)\./);
                    if (a)
                        return +a[1] < 40 ? 'L' + a[1] : 'D' + (+a[1] - 40);
                    a = n.match(/^mtp\.(?:layers\.)?(\d+)\./);
                    if (a)
                        return 'D' + a[1];
                    if (/^(mtp\.|dspark\.)/.test(n))
                        return 'DSpark unassigned';
                    if (/^(embed\.|embed_tokens\.)/.test(n))
                        return 'embed';
                    if (/^(head\.|lm_head\.|norm\.)/.test(n))
                        return 'head';
                    if (/^(vision\.|vision_model\.|vision_encoder\.)/.test(n))
                        return 'vision';
                    if (/^(aligner\.|projector\.|image_)/.test(n))
                        return 'aligner';
                    return 'Unmapped';
                }
                async function auditSafetensors(file) {
                    if (file.size < 10)
                        throw Error(file.name + ': too short for a Safetensors header.');
                    const lead = await file.slice(0, 8).arrayBuffer()
                      , n = new DataView(lead).getBigUint64(0, true);
                    if (n < 2n || n > 67108864n || Number(n) > file.size - 8)
                        throw Error(file.name + ': invalid or unusually large header (limit 64 MiB).');
                    let header;
                    try {
                        header = JSON.parse(await file.slice(8, 8 + Number(n)).text());
                    } catch (e) {
                        throw Error(file.name + ': header is not valid JSON.');
                    }
                    const base = 8 + Number(n)
                      , intervals = []
                      , groups = Object.create(null)
                      , dtypes = Object.create(null)
                      , tensors = [];
                    let payload = 0;
                    for (const [name,w] of Object.entries(header)) {
                        if (name === '__metadata__')
                            continue;
                        if (!w || !Array.isArray(w.data_offsets) || w.data_offsets.length !== 2 || !Array.isArray(w.shape) || typeof w.dtype !== 'string')
                            throw Error('Invalid tensor descriptor: ' + name);
                        const [start,end] = w.data_offsets;
                        if (!Number.isSafeInteger(start) || !Number.isSafeInteger(end) || start < 0 || end < start || base + end > file.size)
                            throw Error('Out-of-bounds tensor: ' + name);
                        const b = end - start;
                        intervals.push([start, end]);
                        payload += b;
                        const group = resolveAuditModule(name);
                        groups[group] = (groups[group] || 0) + b;
                        dtypes[w.dtype] = (dtypes[w.dtype] || 0) + b;
                        tensors.push({
                            name,
                            dtype: w.dtype,
                            shape: w.shape,
                            bytes: b,
                            module: group
                        });
                    }
                    intervals.sort( (a, b) => a[0] - b[0]);
                    let end = 0;
                    for (const r of intervals) {
                        if (r[0] < end && r[1] > r[0])
                            throw Error(file.name + ': overlapping tensor ranges.');
                        end = Math.max(end, r[1]);
                    }
                    return {
                        name: file.name,
                        fileBytes: file.size,
                        headerBytes: base,
                        payloadBytes: payload,
                        otherBytes: file.size - base - payload,
                        groups,
                        dtypes,
                        tensors
                    };
                }
                function fontPreference() {
                    try {
                        const n = Number(localStorage.getItem('deepseek-atlas-font'));
                        return Number.isFinite(n) && n >= .85 && n <= 1.5 ? n : 1;
                    } catch (_) {
                        return 1;
                    }
                }
                // Only actual blank surface collapses a panel. Text, controls, tabs and selection are protected.
                function isBlankPanelPoint(e) {
                    const root = e.currentTarget
                      , el = e.target;
                    if (!(el instanceof Element) || el.closest('button,a,input,select,textarea,summary,label,[role="button"],[role="tab"],[role="tablist"],[contenteditable="true"],.panel-tabs,.inspector-nav,.inspector-top,.inspector-foot,.timeline,.transport-row,.world-labels,svg'))
                        return false;
                    const edge = root.getBoundingClientRect();
                    if (e.clientX > edge.right - 13)
                        return false;
                    // never hijack the scrollbar
                    const tree = document.createTreeWalker(el, NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);
                    let text;
                    while ((text = tree.nextNode())) {
                        if (!text.textContent.trim())
                            continue;
                        const r = document.createRange();
                        r.selectNodeContents(text);
                        for (const b of r.getClientRects())
                            if (e.clientX >= b.left - 3 && e.clientX <= b.right + 3 && e.clientY >= b.top - 2 && e.clientY <= b.bottom + 2)
                                return false;
                    }
                    return true;
                }
                class App extends Component {
                    constructor() {
                        super();
                        this.state = {
                            layout: 'split',
                            activity: 'inference',
                            loopPhase: true,
                            panelHidden: false,
                            timelineHidden: innerWidth < 700 || innerHeight < 600,
                            fontScale: fontPreference(),
                            mobile: innerWidth <= 1100,
                            view: 'architecture',
                            precision: 'native',
                            selected: null,
                            tensor: null,
                            hoverTensor: null,
                            expert: null,
                            detailLayout: 'column',
                            expertView: false,
                            tab: 'inspect',
                            playing: !matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches,
                            motion: false,
                            speed: 1,
                            time: 9,
                            context: 1048576,
                            binary: false,
                            labels: false,
                            links: true,
                            orbit: false,
                            modal: null,
                            sheet: false,
                            filter: 'all',
                            limit: 14,
                            bench: 1,
                            effort: 100,
                            error: null,
                            audit: null,
                            auditing: false,
                            auditError: null,
                            toast: null
                        };
                        this.header = createRef();
                        this.canvas = createRef();
                        this.labels = createRef();
                        this.tip = createRef();
                        this.fileRef = createRef();
                        this.panelRef = createRef();
                        this.engine = null;
                        this.key = this.key.bind(this);
                        this.resizeUI = () => this.patch({
                            mobile: innerWidth <= 1100
                        });
                        this.panelPress = null;
                    }
                    componentDidMount() {
                        if (this.header.current) {
                            const update = () => this.header.current.parentElement.style.setProperty('--header-height', this.header.current.getBoundingClientRect().height + 'px');
                            this.headerObserver = new ResizeObserver(update);
                            this.headerObserver.observe(this.header.current);
                            update();
                        }
                        try {
                            this.engine = new AtlasEngine(this.canvas.current,this.labels.current,this.tip.current,{
                                select: (id, cat, expert) => this.select(id, cat, expert),
                                hover: name => {
                                    if (this.state.hoverTensor !== name)
                                        this.setState({
                                            hoverTensor: name
                                        });
                                }
                                ,
                                tick: time => {
                                    if (Math.abs(this.state.time - time) > .02)
                                        this.setState({
                                            time
                                        });
                                }
                                ,
                                error: error => this.setState({
                                    error
                                })
                            },this.state);
                        } catch (e) {
                            console.error(e);
                            this.setState({
                                error: e.message
                            });
                        }
                        window.addEventListener('keydown', this.key);
                        window.addEventListener('resize', this.resizeUI);
                        window.ATLAS_DEBUG = {
                            app: this,
                            get engine() {
                                return window.ATLAS_DEBUG.app.engine;
                            },
                            CFG,
                            NV_CONFIG,
                            TOTALS,
                            TOTAL_P,
                            MODULES,
                            CATEGORIES,
                            wBytes,
                            sumB
                        };
                    }
                    componentDidUpdate(prevProps, prevState) {
                        if (prevState && prevState.fontScale !== this.state.fontScale) {
                            try {
                                localStorage.setItem('deepseek-atlas-font', String(this.state.fontScale));
                            } catch (_) {}
                        }
                        if (this.engine)
                            this.engine.sync(this.state);
                        if (prevState && (prevState.selected !== this.state.selected || prevState.view !== this.state.view || prevState.tab !== this.state.tab) && this.panelRef.current)
                            this.panelRef.current.scrollTop = 0;
                        if (prevState && prevState.modal !== this.state.modal) {
                            if (this.state.modal) {
                                this.modalReturnFocus = document.activeElement;
                                setTimeout( () => document.querySelector('.modal-head button')?.focus(), 0);
                            } else if (this.modalReturnFocus && document.contains(this.modalReturnFocus))
                                this.modalReturnFocus.focus({
                                    preventScroll: true
                                });
                        }
                    }
                    componentWillUnmount() {
                        if (this.headerObserver)
                            this.headerObserver.disconnect();
                        if (this.engine)
                            this.engine.dispose();
                        window.removeEventListener('keydown', this.key);
                        window.removeEventListener('resize', this.resizeUI);
                        clearTimeout(this.toastTimer);
                    }
                    patch(p) {
                        this.setState(p);
                    }
                    changeFont(delta) {
                        this.patch({
                            fontScale: Math.round(clamp(this.state.fontScale + delta, .85, 1.5) * 100) / 100
                        });
                    }
                    setLayout(layout) {
                        this.patch({
                            layout,
                            view: 'architecture'
                        });
                    }
                    setActivity(activity) {
                        this.patch({
                            activity,
                            loopPhase: activity === 'inference'
                        });
                        this.seek(activity === 'inference' ? 9 : 0);
                    }
                    togglePanel(force) {
                        const open = force === undefined ? (this.state.mobile ? !this.state.sheet : this.state.panelHidden) : force;
                        this.patch({
                            panelHidden: !open,
                            sheet: open && this.state.mobile
                        });
                    }
                    hidePanel() {
                        this.patch({
                            panelHidden: true,
                            sheet: false
                        });
                    }
                    blankMove(e, axis) {
                        if (e.pointerType === 'touch')
                            return;
                        const yes = isBlankPanelPoint(e);
                        e.currentTarget.classList.toggle('blank-' + axis, yes);
                    }
                    blankDown(e, axis) {
                        if (e.pointerType !== 'mouse' || e.button !== 0 || !isBlankPanelPoint(e)) {
                            this.panelPress = null;
                            return;
                        }
                        this.panelPress = {
                            axis,
                            x: e.clientX,
                            y: e.clientY,
                            scroll: e.currentTarget.querySelector('.inspector-scroll')?.scrollTop || 0
                        };
                    }
                    blankUp(e, axis) {
                        const p = this.panelPress;
                        this.panelPress = null;
                        if (!p || p.axis !== axis || Math.hypot(p.x - e.clientX, p.y - e.clientY) > 4 || !isBlankPanelPoint(e) || getSelection()?.toString())
                            return;
                        if ((e.currentTarget.querySelector('.inspector-scroll')?.scrollTop || 0) !== p.scroll)
                            return;
                        if (axis === 'right')
                            this.hidePanel();
                        else
                            this.patch({
                                timelineHidden: true
                            });
                    }

                    notify(toast) {
                        clearTimeout(this.toastTimer);
                        this.setState({
                            toast
                        });
                        this.toastTimer = setTimeout( () => this.setState({
                            toast: null
                        }), 3200);
                    }
                    setView(view) {
                        this.patch({
                            view,
                            selected: null,
                            tensor: null,
                            hoverTensor: null,
                            expert: null,
                            tab: 'inspect',
                            limit: 100
                        });
                    }
                    select(id, tensor=null, expert=null) {
                        if (!id) {
                            this.patch({
                                selected: null,
                                tensor: null,
                                hoverTensor: null,
                                expert: null,
                                limit: 100
                            });
                            return;
                        }
                        const same = this.state.selected === id;
                        if (same && !tensor && (expert === null || expert === undefined) && this.state.view === 'architecture') {
                            this.patch({
                                selected: null,
                                tensor: null,
                                hoverTensor: null,
                                expert: null
                            });
                            return;
                        }
                        this.patch({
                            selected: id,
                            tensor,
                            hoverTensor: null,
                            expert: expert === undefined ? null : expert,
                            filter: 'all',
                            limit: 100,
                            tab: 'inspect',
                            sheet: !!tensor && innerWidth < 1100 ? true : this.state.sheet
                        });
                    }
                    walk(d) {
                        let i = MODULES.findIndex(m => m.id === this.state.selected);
                        i = clamp(i + d, 0, MODULES.length - 1);
                        this.patch({
                            selected: MODULES[i].id,
                            view: 'architecture',
                            tab: 'inspect',
                            tensor: null,
                            expert: null,
                            filter: 'all',
                            limit: 14
                        });
                    }
                    seek(time) {
                        if (this.engine)
                            this.engine.seek(time);
                        else
                            this.patch({
                                time
                            });
                    }
                    key(e) {
                        if (e.ctrlKey || e.metaKey || e.altKey)
                            return;
                        const k = e.key.toLowerCase();
                        if (k === 'escape') {
                            if (this.state.modal || this.state.sheet)
                                this.patch({
                                    modal: null,
                                    sheet: false
                                });
                            else if (this.state.tensor || this.state.expert !== null)
                                this.patch({
                                    tensor: null,
                                    hoverTensor: null,
                                    expert: null
                                });
                            else
                                this.patch({
                                    selected: null
                                });
                            return;
                        }
                        if (this.state.modal) {
                            if (k === 'tab') {
                                let nodes = [...document.querySelectorAll('.modal button:not(:disabled),.modal a,.modal input:not([hidden]),.modal select,.modal summary')].filter(x => x.getClientRects().length)
                                  , first = nodes[0]
                                  , last = nodes[nodes.length - 1];
                                if (e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === first) {
                                    e.preventDefault();
                                    last?.focus();
                                } else if (!e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last) {
                                    e.preventDefault();
                                    first?.focus();
                                }
                            }
                            return;
                        }
                        if (/input|select|textarea/i.test(e.target.tagName) || e.target.tagName === 'BUTTON' && k === ' ')
                            return;
                        if (k === ' ') {
                            e.preventDefault();
                            this.patch({
                                playing: !this.state.playing
                            });
                        }
                        if (k === 'arrowdown') {
                            e.preventDefault();
                            this.walk(1);
                        }
                        if (k === 'arrowup') {
                            e.preventDefault();
                            this.walk(-1);
                        }
                        if (k === '+' || k === '=') {
                            e.preventDefault();
                            this.changeFont(.1);
                        }
                        if (k === '-') {
                            e.preventDefault();
                            this.changeFont(-.1);
                        }
                        if (k === 'b')
                            this.togglePanel();
                        if (k === 't')
                            this.patch({
                                timelineHidden: !this.state.timelineHidden
                            });
                        if (k === 'v')
                            this.setLayout(this.state.layout === 'tower' ? 'split' : 'tower');
                        if (k === 'r' && this.engine) {
                            if (this.state.selected)
                                this.engine.focus(this.state.selected);
                            else
                                this.engine.reset();
                        }
                        if (k === 'f')
                            this.patch({
                                detailLayout: this.state.detailLayout === 'floor' ? 'column' : 'floor'
                            });
                        if (k === 'x')
                            this.patch({
                                expertView: !this.state.expertView,
                                expert: null
                            });
                        if (k === '[')
                            this.walkTensor(-1);
                        if (k === ']')
                            this.walkTensor(1);
                        if (k === 'p') {
                            let keys = ['native', 'nvfp4', 'bf16'];
                            this.patch({
                                precision: keys[(keys.indexOf(this.state.precision) + 1) % 3]
                            });
                        }
                        if (k === 'l')
                            this.patch({
                                labels: !this.state.labels
                            });
                        if (k === 'k')
                            this.patch({
                                links: !this.state.links
                            });
                        if (['1', '2', '3'].includes(k))
                            this.setView(['architecture', 'storage', 'cache'][Number(k) - 1]);
                    }

                    exportLedger() {
                        let mode = this.state.precision;
                        safeDownload('deepseek-v41-' + mode + '-derived-ledger.json', JSON.stringify({
                            title: 'DeepSeek V4.1 Flash - schema-derived payload ledger',
                            date: '2026-09-10',
                            precision: mode,
                            status: 'DERIVED, NOT SHARD-AUDITED',
                            notes: ['Not device VRAM. Headers, padding and unknown auxiliary buffers are not included.', 'NVFP4 uses the user-supplied backbone-routed-expert-only recipe.', 'NVFP4 tensor-level scale overhead is modeled as 8 bytes per expert matrix; actual exporter metadata can differ.', 'BF16 is a hypothetical two-byte-per-parameter reference, not a published artifact.'],
                            bytes: TOTALS[mode],
                            logicalParameters: TOTAL_P,
                            modules: MODULES.map(m => ({
                                id: m.id,
                                label: m.label,
                                bytes: sumB(m.ws, mode),
                                parameters: sumP(m.ws),
                                weights: m.ws.map(w => ({
                                    ...w,
                                    storageBytes: wBytes(w, mode),
                                    displayFormat: wFormat(w, mode)
                                }))
                            })),
                            sources: SOURCES
                        }, null, 2));
                        this.notify('Byte ledger exported. Estimates are explicitly labeled.');
                    }
                    snapshot() {
                        if (!this.engine)
                            return;
                        const a = document.createElement('a');
                        a.href = this.engine.snapshot();
                        a.download = 'deepseek-v41-3d-' + this.state.view + '.png';
                        a.click();
                        this.notify('3D canvas saved. Interface labels are not part of the canvas.');
                    }
                    async audit(files) {
                        if (!files.length)
                            return;
                        this.patch({
                            auditing: true,
                            auditError: null,
                            audit: null
                        });
                        try {
                            let results = [];
                            for (const file of files) {
                                if (!file.name.endsWith('.safetensors'))
                                    throw Error('Select .safetensors shards, not the index JSON.');
                                results.push(await auditSafetensors(file));
                                this.patch({
                                    auditProgress: results.length + ' / ' + files.length
                                });
                            }
                            const names = new Set;
                            for (const r of results)
                                for (const t of r.tensors) {
                                    if (names.has(t.name))
                                        throw Error('Duplicate tensor ' + t.name + '. Select one checkpoint, without duplicate shards.');
                                    names.add(t.name);
                                }
                            const groups = Object.create(null)
                              , dtypes = Object.create(null);
                            let payload = 0
                              , fileBytes = 0
                              , headerBytes = 0;
                            for (const r of results) {
                                payload += r.payloadBytes;
                                fileBytes += r.fileBytes;
                                headerBytes += r.headerBytes;
                                for (const [k,v] of Object.entries(r.groups))
                                    groups[k] = (groups[k] || 0) + v;
                                for (const [k,v] of Object.entries(r.dtypes))
                                    dtypes[k] = (dtypes[k] || 0) + v;
                            }
                            this.patch({
                                audit: {
                                    results,
                                    groups,
                                    dtypes,
                                    payload,
                                    fileBytes,
                                    headerBytes,
                                    tensorCount: names.size,
                                    mode: this.state.precision
                                },
                                auditing: false
                            });
                        } catch (e) {
                            this.patch({
                                auditError: e.message,
                                auditing: false
                            });
                        }
                        if (this.fileRef.current)
                            this.fileRef.current.value = '';
                    }
                    renderOverview() {
                        const s = this.state;
                        return html`<div>
  <div class="intro-kicker">ONE CHECKPOINT / ONE CONTINUOUS FORWARD PASS</div><h2 class="editorial">All 40 layers.<br/><em>Every decode pass.</em></h2><p class="lede">The two groups are consecutive, not alternative. A normal decode pass goes through the encoder and then the decoder.</p>
  <div class="decode-path" aria-label="Decode execution order"><span>Embedding</span><i>\u2192</i><span>L00\u2013L19<small>encoder</small></span><i>\u2192</i><span>L20\u2013L39<small>decoder</small></span><i>\u2192</i><span>Head</span></div>
  <${Section} title="Same block pattern, different weights"><p class="fine">Both groups use attention, residual mixing and an MoE feed-forward block. The matrices can have matching shapes, but <b>L19 and L20 have their own learned values</b>. The model does not swap checkpoints or skip the second group when generating the next token.</p><p class="fine">The split is a layout for reading the architecture. <b>The actual layer order remains L00 through L39.</b> The tower shows that same sequence vertically; neither layout maps physical shard offsets.</p><button class="text-link" onClick=${ () => this.setLayout(s.layout === 'tower' ? 'split' : 'tower')}>${s.layout === 'tower' ? 'Separate the two layer ranges' : 'Merge into one numbered tower'} <${Icon} name="arrow" size=${14}/></button><//>
  <${Section} title="What actually changes at the boundary?"><p class="fine">The decoder's global attention reads a shared bank projected from the final encoder representation. Each decoder layer still computes its own queries, local-window attention and expert output. <b>Sharing cached activations does not mean sharing all weights or skipping layers.</b></p><p class="fine">The published <a href=${SOURCE_BASE + '/blob/main/inference/model.py'} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reference forward function</a> iterates over the complete backbone before the output head. Layer cache-owner and index-owner settings are fixed architecture, not switches flipped halfway through a token.</p><//>
  <details class="assumptions"><summary>Then why does prefill sometimes show only half?</summary><p>Inference includes prompt preparation as well as token decoding. The model card's optimized CED prefill builds global memory from the encoder and reconstructs bounded local-window state. This avoids a full decoder pass over every cached prompt position; it does not remove the decoder from normal token generation.</p><p>The minimal reference implementation instead runs its complete backbone loop for both prompt and decode inputs. The Prefill* animation illustrates the optimized schedule, not a literal trace of that loop.</p><a href=${SOURCE_BASE + '#introduction'} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Read the model-card architecture description</a></details>

  <div class="stat-grid"><div><small>BACKBONE</small><strong>552<span>B</span></strong></div><div><small>ENGRAM TABLES</small><strong>196.6<span>B</span></strong></div><div><small>PREFILL / TOKEN</small><strong>8<span>B active</span></strong></div><div><small>DECODE / TOKEN</small><strong>16<span>B active</span></strong></div></div>
  <${Note}>The model card describes an optimized prefill: process the full prompt with L00-L19, project decoder global KV, then perform bounded local-window replay. New generated tokens use all 40 backbone layers. That is an execution optimization, not a different set of weights.<//>
  <${Section} title="Read the architecture / click to unfold" tag="40 backbone layers"><div class="mode-legend">${[['full', '4', 'Full'], ['reindex', '4', 'Reindex'], ['reuse', '30', 'Reuse'], ['swa', '2', 'SWA only']].map( ([key,n,label]) => html`<button class="mode-key" onClick=${ () => this.select(LAYERS.find(l => l.mode === key).id)}><i style=${{
                            background: COL[key]
                        }}/><b>${n}</b><span>${label}</span></button>`)}</div>
   <${Fact} label="Hidden width / mHC streams" value="5,120 / 4"/><${Fact} label="Attention heads / head width" value="64 / 512"/><${Fact} label="Experts at every layer" value="384 routed + 1 shared"/><${Fact} label="Routed experts used / token" value="6 of 384"/><${Fact} label="Local sliding window" value="128 tokens"/><${Fact} label="Global KV owners" value="L02 / L08 / L14 / L20" color=${COL.full}/><p class="fine">Full means new KV and sparse indices, not dense global attention. All layer IDs are zero-based.</p><//>
  <button class="outline-btn full-width" onClick=${ () => this.patch({
                            tab: 'guide'
                        })}>Guided tour: Full, Reindex and Reuse <${Icon} name="arrow" size=${14}/></button><${Section} title="Beyond the backbone">${[['E1', 'E', 'Engram conditional memory', 'Two enormous tables. Sparse n-gram lookup.', COL.engram], ['vision', 'V', 'DeepSeek-ViT + projector', '32 vision layers / 3 x 3 unshuffle', COL.vision], ['D0', 'D', 'DSpark speculative draft', 'Three stages / five-token blocks', COL.mhc]].map( ([id,letter,title,sub,c]) => html`<button class="feature-link" onClick=${ () => this.select(id)}><span class="feature-icon" style=${{
                            color: c
                        }}>${letter}</span><span><b>${title}</b><small>${sub}</small></span><${Icon} name="chevron" size=${14}/></button>`)}<//>
  <${Section} title="What the volumes mean" tag="Storage, not VRAM"><${Fact} label="Logical values in this inventory" value=${fmtP(TOTAL_P)}/><${Fact} label=${MODE_INFO[s.precision].label + ' payload'} value=${bytes(TOTALS[s.precision], s.binary)} color=${MODE_INFO[s.precision].color}/><p class="fine">Solids use one common scale: 1 cubic world unit = 1 GB. Empty space, paths, particles and labels are schematic. Payloads are derived from shapes and formats, not certified by all shard headers.</p><button class="text-link" onClick=${ () => this.setView('storage')}>Inspect the byte ledger <${Icon} name="arrow" size=${15}/></button><//>
 </div>`;
                    }
                    walkTensor(d) {
                        const m = MODULES.find(x => x.id === this.state.selected);
                        if (!m)
                            return;
                        const ws = orderWeights(m);
                        let i = ws.findIndex(w => w.name === this.state.tensor);
                        i = i < 0 ? (d > 0 ? 0 : ws.length - 1) : (i + d + ws.length) % ws.length;
                        this.patch({
                            tensor: ws[i].name,
                            hoverTensor: null,
                            filter: 'all',
                            limit: 100
                        });
                    }
                    renderInsight(m, w) {
                        const s = this.state
                          , single = s.expert !== null && s.expert !== undefined && w.cat === 'expert'
                          , v = single ? {
                            ...w,
                            count: 1,
                            p: w.p / w.count
                        } : w
                          , info = tensorInfo(v, m)
                          , b = weightParts(v, s.precision)
                          , pinned = s.tensor === w.name;
                        return html`<section class="tensor-insight" style=${{
                            '--tensor': tensorColor(w)
                        }} aria-label="Tensor explanation"><div class="insight-kicker"><span>${pinned ? 'PINNED WEIGHT' : 'HOVERED WEIGHT'}${single ? ' / EXPERT ' + s.expert : ''}</span><button class="tiny-btn" onClick=${ () => this.patch({
                            tensor: null,
                            hoverTensor: null
                        })} title="Unpin tensor">${pinned ? 'Unpin' : 'Clear'}</button></div><h3>${info.title}</h3><code class="insight-name">${w.name}</code><p>${info.job}</p><div class="tensor-equation">${info.flow}</div><p>${info.why}</p>${info.caution && html`<div class="caution-small">${info.caution}</div>`}
  <div class="tensor-numbers"><div><small>LOGICAL SHAPE</small><strong>[${w.shape.map(num).join(', ')}]${v.count > 1 ? ' \u00d7 ' + v.count : ''}</strong></div><div><small>MODELED FORMAT</small><strong>${wFormat(w, s.precision)}</strong></div></div>
  <details class="account-details"><summary>${bytes(b.total, s.binary)} stored payload <span>Show the arithmetic</span></summary><${Fact} label="Logical parameters" value=${fmtP(v.p)}/><${Fact} label="Packed data" value=${bytes(b.data, s.binary)}/><${Fact} label="Group / block scales" value=${b.scales ? bytes(b.scales, s.binary) : 'None in this model'}/>${b.aux > 0 && html`<${Fact} label="Tensor-level scales (estimate)" value=${num(b.aux) + ' B'}/>`}<${Fact} label="Total" value=${num(b.total) + ' B'}/><p class="fine">Matrix shapes are [output, input]. Volumes include modeled scales. Exact checkpoint dtypes, metadata and padding require a shard-header audit.</p></details>
  <div class="insight-actions"><button class="text-link" onClick=${ () => this.engine && this.engine.focusTensor(w.name)}>Zoom to this tensor <${Icon} name="expand" size=${13}/></button><a href=${SOURCE_BASE + '/blob/main/inference/' + (info.source === 'vision' ? 'vision.py' : 'model.py')} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">Reference code \u2197</a></div>
 </section>`;
                    }
                    renderModule(m) {
                        const s = this.state
                          , ws = displayWeights(m)
                          , story = layerStory(m)
                          , exp = moduleExplanation(m)
                          , isLayer = !!m.mode || m.id.startsWith('D')
                          , isExpert = s.expert !== null && s.expert !== undefined
                          , focus = findWeight(m, s.hoverTensor || s.tensor)
                          , cc = m.part === 'encoder' ? COL.enc : m.part === 'decoder' ? COL.dec : m.id[0] === 'E' ? TC.engram : m.id[0] === 'D' ? TC.mhc : TC.vision;
                        let weights = orderWeights(m).filter(w => s.filter === 'all' || w.cat === s.filter);
                        if (isExpert)
                            weights = ws.filter(w => w.cat === 'expert');
                        const cats = [...new Set(ws.map(w => w.cat))];
                        return html`<div><div class="inspect-path">${m.part ? m.part.toUpperCase() : 'AUXILIARY MODULE'} <span>/ ${m.id}${isExpert ? ' / expert ' + s.expert : ''}</span></div><div class="module-title"><h2>${m.label}</h2>${m.mode && html`<${Chip} color=${COL[m.mode]}>${m.mode === 'swa' ? 'SWA only' : m.mode}<//>`}</div>
 <div class="module-metrics"><div><small>LOGICAL PARAMETERS</small><b>${fmtP(isExpert ? MOE_EXPERT_P : sumP(m.ws))}</b></div><div><small>MODELED PAYLOAD</small><b style=${{
                            color: cc
                        }}>${bytes(isExpert ? sumB(m.ws.filter(w => w.cat === 'expert'), s.precision) / (m.id[0] === 'D' ? 128 : 384) : sumB(m.ws, s.precision), s.binary)}</b></div></div>
 <div class="tensor-walk"><button aria-label="Previous tensor" onClick=${ () => this.walkTensor(-1)}><${Icon} name="up" size=${13}/></button><span>${s.tensor ? 'Follow this layer\u2019s weights' : 'Click a labeled weight to explain it'}<small>${ws.length} descriptors / arrows walk every tensor</small></span><button aria-label="Next tensor" onClick=${ () => this.walkTensor(1)}><${Icon} name="down" size=${13}/></button></div>
 ${focus && this.renderInsight(m, focus)}
 ${m.mode && html`<p class="layer-execution"><b>Decode step ${m.index + 1} of 40.</b> ${m.index === 19 ? 'The next layer is L20; the forward pass continues into the decoder.' : m.index === 20 ? 'Runs after L19, using its own attention and expert weights.' : m.index === 39 ? 'The output head follows this final backbone layer.' : 'Runs in sequence; its cache mode does not skip its computation.'}</p>`}
 ${story ? html`<${Section} title="What this layer actually does"><div class="layer-story"><h3>${story.title}</h3><p>${story.body}</p><p>${story.detail}</p></div><//>` : html`<${Note} color=${cc}><strong>${exp.title}</strong> ${exp.body}<//>`}
 ${m.mode && html`<${Section} title="Computed here or borrowed?" tag="WEIGHTS VS RUNTIME STATE"><div class="ownership">
 ${[['Main attention queries', 'Own weights', 'All 64 main queries are computed here.', TC.q, 'own'], ['Local SWA KV', 'Own weights', 'Own 128-token window at every layer.', TC.local, 'own'], ['Global main KV', m.owner === null ? 'Absent' : m.owner === m.index ? 'Writes bank' : 'From L' + m.owner, m.owner === null ? 'No global branch at layers 0 and 1.' : m.index === 20 ? 'Projected from final encoder states.' : m.owner === m.index ? 'This owner pools token pairs.' : 'Cached KV activations are shared.', TC.kv, m.owner === null ? 'off' : m.owner === m.index ? 'own' : 'borrow'], ['Indexer keys', m.owner === null ? 'Absent' : m.owner === m.index ? 'Own projection' : 'From L' + m.owner, m.owner === null ? 'There are no global positions to rank.' : m.owner === m.index ? '128-channel keys from main KV.' : 'No indexer key projection in this layer.', TC.index, m.owner === null ? 'off' : m.owner === m.index ? 'own' : 'borrow'], ['Top-512 selection', m.indexOwner === null ? 'Absent' : m.indexOwner === m.index ? 'Recomputed' : 'From L' + m.indexOwner, m.mode === 'reindex' ? 'New query; search L20\u2019s candidate pool.' : m.indexOwner === m.index ? 'Rank visible positions; select a sparse set.' : m.indexOwner === null ? 'Local attention only.' : 'No fresh sparse-index query matrix here.', TC.index, m.indexOwner === null ? 'off' : m.indexOwner === m.index ? 'own' : 'borrow']].map( ([name,status,desc,c,kind]) => html`<div class="ownership-row"><i class=${kind} style=${{
                            '--own': c
                        }}/><div><b>${name}</b><small>${desc}</small></div><span style=${{
                            color: kind === 'off' ? '#63768e' : c
                        }}>${status}</span></div>`)}</div>
 ${m.owner !== null && m.owner !== m.index && html`<button class="text-link" onClick=${ () => this.select('L' + m.owner)}>Open the source layer L${m.owner} <${Icon} name="arrow" size=${14}/></button>`}
 <p class="fine">Borrowed objects are runtime memory and position indices, not duplicated parameter tensors. Their absence from this layer\u2019s weight list is intentional.</p><//>`}
 ${isLayer && html`<${Section} title="Inside one expert" tag=${m.id[0] === 'D' ? '128 / TOP-3' : '384 / TOP-6'}><div class="expert-equation">gate = SiLU(W1 x)<br/>content = W3 x<br/>output = W2(gate \u2299 content)</div><${Fact} label="One expert / logical parameters" value=${fmtP(MOE_EXPERT_P)}/><${Fact} label="Expert width" value="5,120 \u2192 2,304 \u2192 5,120"/><${Fact} label="Three weight matrices / expert" value=${bytes(sumB(m.ws.filter(w => w.cat === 'expert'), s.precision) / (m.id[0] === 'D' ? 128 : 384), s.binary)}/><p class="fine">A bank marked \u00d7${m.id[0] === 'D' ? 128 : 384} contains that many separate matrices. The shared expert is always used; only the selected routed experts are evaluated. Stored volume does not shrink when an expert is idle.</p><button class="outline-btn full-width" onClick=${ () => this.patch({
                            expertView: !s.expertView,
                            expert: null,
                            tensor: null,
                            hoverTensor: null
                        })}>${s.expertView ? 'Return to named tensor banks' : 'Unfold all individual experts'} <${Icon} name="expand" size=${14}/></button><//>`}
 ${m.id[0] === 'E' && html`<${Section} title="From token IDs to a gated lookup"><${Fact} label="Orders / hash heads" value="2, 3, 4 / 8 each"/><${Fact} label="Rows fetched per token" value="24 per module"/><${Fact} label="Retrieved channels" value="24 \u00d7 256 = 6,144"/><${Fact} label="Combined key/value projection" value="6,144 \u2192 25,600"/><p class="fine">The projection produces four keys and one value. The residual streams gate how much of that value they accept. Sparse lookup makes the accessed portion small; all table values still contribute to stored bytes.</p><//>`}
 ${(m.id === 'vision' || m.id === 'aligner') && html`<${Section} title="Images meet the language model"><div class="expert-equation">14 \u00d7 14 RGB patches<br/>\u2193 32 vision blocks, width 1,024<br/>3 \u00d7 3 unshuffle: 9,216 channels<br/>\u2193 two-layer projector<br/>5,120-channel visual embeddings</div><p class="fine">In the vision scene, the same tensor role across 32 blocks is combined into a bank marked \u00d732. These are 32 separately stored copies, not shared weights.</p><//>`}
 <${Section} title=${isExpert ? 'Expert ' + String(s.expert).padStart(3, '0') + ' / three matrices' : 'Every named tensor'} tag=${ws.length + ' DESCRIPTORS'}><div class="filter-row"><label>Show <select aria-label="Tensor category" value=${isExpert ? 'expert' : s.filter} onChange=${e => this.patch({
                            filter: e.target.value,
                            expert: null,
                            limit: 100
                        })}><option value="all">All tensors</option>${cats.map(c => html`<option value=${c}>${c}</option>`)}</select></label>${(s.filter !== 'all' || isExpert) && html`<button class="tiny-btn" onClick=${ () => this.patch({
                            filter: 'all',
                            expert: null,
                            limit: 100
                        })}>Clear</button>`}</div>
 ${weights.slice(0, s.limit).map(w => {
                            const div = isExpert ? w.count : 1
                              , hot = (s.hoverTensor || s.tensor) === w.name;
                            return html`<button key=${w.name} class=${'weight-button' + (hot ? ' hot' : '')} style=${{
                                '--tensor': tensorColor(w)
                            }} aria-pressed=${s.tensor === w.name} title="Pin the tensor explanation and highlight its solid" onMouseEnter=${ () => this.patch({
                                hoverTensor: w.name
                            })} onMouseLeave=${ () => this.patch({
                                hoverTensor: null
                            })} onClick=${ () => {
                                this.patch({
                                    tensor: w.name,
                                    hoverTensor: null
                                });
                                if (this.panelRef.current)
                                    this.panelRef.current.scrollTo({
                                        top: 0,
                                        behavior: matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches ? 'auto' : 'smooth'
                                    });
                            }
                            }><i/><span><b>${w.name}</b><small>[${w.shape.map(num).join(', ')}]${w.count > 1 && !isExpert ? ' \u00d7 ' + w.count : ''}</small><em>${wFormat(w, s.precision)} / ${fmtP(w.p / div)} parameters</em></span><strong>${bytes(wBytes(w, s.precision) / div, s.binary)}</strong></button>`;
                        }
                        )}
 ${weights.length > s.limit && html`<button class="outline-btn full-width" onClick=${ () => this.patch({
                            limit: s.limit + 40
                        })}>Show ${weights.length - s.limit} more tensors</button>`}<p class="fine">Click any row or scene label for the operation, input/output shape, and byte arithmetic. Tiny pick dots and leader lines are annotations, not extra stored mass.</p><//>
 <${Section} title="Same weights, different payload">${Object.keys(MODE_INFO).map(mode => {
                            const b = sumB(m.ws, mode);
                            return html`<div class="ledger-bar"><div><span>${MODE_INFO[mode].label}</span><b>${bytes(b, s.binary)}</b></div><span><i style=${{
                                width: b / sumB(m.ws, 'bf16') * 100 + '%',
                                background: MODE_INFO[mode].color,
                                opacity: mode === s.precision ? 1 : .35
                            }}/></span></div>`;
                        }
                        )}<p class="fine">NVFP4 only changes the explicitly targeted backbone routed experts. Ignored tensors retain their source dtypes. BF16 is a hypothetical reference, not a published checkpoint.</p><//></div>`;
                    }
                    renderGuide() {
                        return html`<div><div class="inspect-path">A READING GUIDE TO THE ANIMATION</div><h2 class="editorial">Follow the data.<br/><em>Read the weights.</em></h2><p class="lede">The solids are learned tensors. The moving lights are explanatory signals. Do not read the size of a weight as its runtime workload.</p>
 <${Section} title="Start with a Reuse layer"><p class="guide-copy">Open L03. Its own query matrices ask questions of memory made at L02. It still has local KV, output projections and a full expert bank. The missing compressor and indexer are not a broken expansion: their cached results are borrowed.</p><button class="outline-btn full-width" onClick=${ () => {
                            this.select('L3');
                            this.patch({
                                tab: 'inspect'
                            });
                        }
                        }>Open L03: see what Reuse keeps</button><//>
 <${Section} title="Compare a Full layer"><p class="guide-copy">Now open L02. The extra compressor weights create a global memory entry for every pair of tokens. The indexer adds search keys, query projections and score weights. Full means these operations exist here; it does not mean dense global attention.</p><button class="outline-btn full-width" onClick=${ () => {
                            this.select('L2');
                            this.patch({
                                tab: 'inspect'
                            });
                        }
                        }>Open L02: see the extra matrices</button><//>
 <${Section} title="Cross the encoder-decoder boundary"><p class="guide-copy">L20\u2019s global KV comes from the encoder output. Bulk prefill can therefore run the first 20 layers and prepare decoder memory without running every decoder layer on every prompt token. A bounded suffix replay reconstructs local SWA state; new decoded tokens traverse both halves.</p><button class="outline-btn full-width" onClick=${ () => {
                            this.select('L20');
                            this.patch({
                                tab: 'inspect'
                            });
                        }
                        }>Open L20: follow the shared memory</button><//>
 <${Section} title="Same memory, a different search"><p class="guide-copy">L24 reuses L20\u2019s KV and search keys but has fresh indexer queries. Its search is restricted to the candidate pool selected at L20: up to 2,048 blocks of eight positions, then Top-512. These are activation indices, not more learned weights.</p><button class="outline-btn full-width" onClick=${ () => {
                            this.select('L24');
                            this.patch({
                                tab: 'inspect'
                            });
                        }
                        }>Open L24: compare Reindex</button><//>
 <${Section} title="Three distinctions worth keeping"><div class="layer-story"><h3>Stored is not active.</h3><p>Only six routed experts are chosen per backbone token, but all 384 specialists remain in the checkpoint. Highlighting an expert changes its light, not its volume.</p><h3>Weights are not the KV cache.</h3><p>A projection matrix is learned and fixed for inference. It produces token-dependent keys and values that accumulate in runtime memory. Reusing those activations does not imply tied query weights.</p><h3>A format label is not a full precision recipe.</h3><p>The native release already has FP4 experts. NVFP4\u2019s more frequent group scales add modeled bytes here. Attention, shared experts, Engram and draft stages are not all converted just because the checkpoint name changes.</p></div><//>
 <${Section} title="A byte-true view, with limits"><p class="guide-copy">Every solid uses 1 cubic world unit per decimal GB of modeled payload. Its three dimensions multiply to that volume, including throughout the morph. Aspect ratio is chosen for readability; it is not the literal matrix shape. Small picking dots, links and labels carry no bytes.</p><p class="guide-copy">The ledger remains schema-derived. A complete set of checkpoint headers is required before calling its totals exact on-disk measurements.</p><button class="text-link" onClick=${ () => this.patch({
                            modal: 'audit'
                        })}>Audit local Safetensors headers <${Icon} name="arrow" size=${14}/></button><//></div>`;
                    }
                    renderStorage() {
                        const s = this.state
                          , selected = s.selected && s.selected.startsWith('cat:') ? CATEGORIES.find(c => 'cat:' + c[0] === s.selected) : null;
                        return html`<div><div class="inspect-path">PARAMETERS / FORMATS / BYTES</div><h2 class="editorial">Storage, <em>honestly.</em></h2><p class="lede">Mass is conserved as the architecture unfolds. Compare the same logical weights in different formats.</p><div class="storage-total"><small>${MODE_INFO[s.precision].note.toUpperCase()}</small><strong>${bytes(TOTALS[s.precision], s.binary)}</strong><span>Schema-derived tensor payload estimate</span></div>
 <${Note} color=${s.precision === 'nvfp4' ? COL.dec : COL.enc}><strong>${s.precision === 'bf16' ? 'A reference, not a checkpoint.' : EXP[s.precision].title}</strong> ${s.precision === 'bf16' ? 'Every logical value is charged two bytes. This baseline is hypothetical, not an available BF16 repository or a device-memory prediction.' : EXP[s.precision].body}<//>
 <${Section} title="Payload by component" tag=${selected ? 'SELECTED: ' + selected[0].toUpperCase() : 'ALL MODULES'}>${CATEGORIES.map( ([id,name,c,ws]) => {
                            let b = sumB(ws, s.precision)
                              , active = selected && selected[0] === id;
                            return html`<button class=${'category-row' + (active ? ' active' : '')} onClick=${ () => this.patch({
                                selected: 'cat:' + id
                            })}><div><i style=${{
                                background: c
                            }}/><span>${name}</span><b>${bytes(b, s.binary)}</b></div><span class="mini-track"><i style=${{
                                width: b / TOTALS[s.precision] * 100 + '%',
                                background: c
                            }}/></span><small>${fmtP(sumP(ws))} parameters <span>${(b / TOTALS[s.precision] * 100).toFixed(2)}%</span></small></button>`;
                        }
                        )}<//>
 <${Section} title="Why NVFP4 grows here"><div class="equation"><span>NATIVE</span><code>0.5 + 1/32 = 0.53125 B/p</code><span>NVFP4</span><code>0.5 + 1/16 = 0.56250 B/p</code></div><${Fact} label="Routed expert payload change" value="+5.88%" color=${COL.full}/><${Fact} label="Modeled checkpoint change" value={'+'+bytes(NV_DELTA,s.binary)} color=${COL.full}/><p class="fine">Only 40 backbone expert banks change. Extra per-tensor scaling is estimated at eight bytes per expert matrix. The supplied recipe does not quantize Engram to FP4.</p><//>
 <${Section} title="Audit, rather than guess"><p class="fine">The 510 GB upstream listing is consistent with this estimate, but rounded. Inspect local Safetensors headers for exact stored tensor bytes, including scale tensors. No weights are loaded or uploaded.</p><button class="outline-btn full-width" onClick=${ () => this.patch({
                            modal: 'audit'
                        })}><${Icon} name="expand" size=${16}/> Audit local checkpoint headers</button><button class="text-link" onClick=${ () => this.exportLedger()}>Export derived tensor ledger <${Icon} name="download" size=${14}/></button><//>
 <details class="assumptions"><summary>Accounting assumptions and limits</summary><p>Safetensors headers, file padding, unknown extra buffers and exporter-specific metadata are not included. The inventory follows the released config and reference shapes. Some checkpoint dtypes, notably compressor weights, require shard-header confirmation.</p><p>FP8 linears include one byte per 32 x 32 scale block. Engram includes one scale byte per 32 values. Expert banks include packed data and group scales. F32 residual mixing and routing correction tensors remain F32 natively.</p><p>The reference converter expands the FP8 grouped output projection <code>wo_a</code> to BF16; the runtime expands the BF16 output head to F32. Neither expansion is counted as disk storage. Serving buffers, tensor parallelism and GPU allocator overhead are different quantities.</p></details></div>`;
                    }
                    renderCache() {
                        const s = this.state
                          , n = s.context
                          , global = n * 890;
                        return html`<div><div class="inspect-path">COMPRESSED SPARSE ATTENTION 2</div><h2 class="editorial">One memory.<br/><em>Many readers.</em></h2><p class="lede">Four global KV owners. Eight indexing layers. Forty local sliding windows.</p><div class="storage-total cache-total"><small>GLOBAL CACHE / ORIGINAL TOKEN</small><strong>890 <em>bytes</em></strong><span>Model-wide, not multiplied by 40 layers</span></div>
 <${Section} title="Stretch the context" tag="UP TO 1,048,576"><label class="range-title" for="context-number">Original input tokens <input id="context-number" type="number" min="128" max="1048576" step="128" value=${n} onChange=${e => {
                            const v = Number(e.target.value);
                            this.patch({
                                context: clamp(Number.isFinite(v) ? Math.round(v) : 128, 128, 1048576)
                            });
                        }
                        }/></label><input class="slider" aria-label="Context length logarithmic slider" type="range" min="7" max="20" step="0.05" value=${Math.log2(n)} onInput=${e => this.patch({
                            context: clamp(Math.round(2 ** Number(e.target.value)), 128, 1048576)
                        })}/><div class="range-ends"><span>128</span><span>16K</span><span>1M</span></div><div class="preset-row">${[16384, 65536, 262144, 1048576].map(v => html`<button class=${n === v ? 'active' : ''} onClick=${ () => this.patch({
                            context: v
                        })}>${v === 1048576 ? '1M' : v / 1024 + 'K'}</button>`)}</div><${Fact} label="Global cache payload" value=${bytes(global, s.binary)} color=${COL.full}/><${Fact} label="Exact 890 x token estimate" value=${num(global) + ' B'}/><p class="fine">The four solids use integer stored positions. At odd sequence lengths they differ by at most 534 B from the 890 x N asymptotic expression.</p><//>
 <${Section} title="Where 890 comes from"><div class="equation"><span>MAIN KV / STORED POSITION</span><code>512 x 0.5 + 512/16 = 288 B</code><span>INDEX K / STORED POSITION</span><code>128 x 0.5 + 128/32 = 68 B</code><span>MODEL / ORIGINAL TOKEN</span><code>(3/2 + 1) x (288 + 68) = 890 B</code></div><p class="fine">Three encoder banks compress pairs of input tokens. The decoder bank keeps one position per input token. Main KV uses E2M1 with an E4M3 scale per 16 channels.</p><//>
 <${Section} title="Hierarchical sparse indexer"><${Fact} label="Candidate source" value="Layer 20"/><${Fact} label="Candidate block selection" value="2,048 x 8 positions"/><${Fact} label="Candidate cap" value="16,384 positions"/><${Fact} label="Final sparse selection" value="Top-512 / query"/><${Fact} label="Deeper reindex layers" value="24, 28, 32, 36"/><p class="fine">The point field is a schematic, not a storage mass. Its illuminated marks illustrate a sparse subset, not measured query indices.</p><//><${Note} color=${COL.full}>This is the production cache format described by the model card and reconstructed from code. Fake-quantized reference tensors need not consume 890 bytes per token. Local SWA state, prefill workspace and allocator overhead are excluded.<//></div>`;
                    }

                        })}>Columns</button><button aria-pressed=${s.detailLayout === 'floor'} class=${s.detailLayout === 'floor' ? 'active' : ''} onClick=${ () => this.patch({
                            detailLayout: 'floor'
                        })}>Floor</button></div>${(selected.mode || selected.id.startsWith('D')) && html`<div class="expansion-switch" role="group" aria-label="Expert representation"><button aria-pressed=${!s.expertView} class=${!s.expertView ? 'active' : ''} onClick=${ () => this.patch({
                            expertView: false,
                            expert: null
                        })}>Tensor banks</button><button aria-pressed=${s.expertView} class=${s.expertView ? 'active' : ''} onClick=${ () => this.patch({
                            expertView: true,
                            expert: null
                        })}>${selected.id.startsWith('D') ? '128' : '384'} experts</button></div>`}<button class="tool-btn fit-text" onClick=${ () => this.engine && this.engine.focus(selected.id)}>Fit layer</button><button class="tool-btn" aria-label="Close layer expansion" onClick=${ () => this.patch({
                            selected: null,
                            tensor: null,
                            hoverTensor: null,
                            expert: null
                        })}><${Icon} name="close" size=${11}/> Close</button></div><div class="expansion-caption">${s.detailLayout === 'floor' && !s.expertView ? '' : s.expertView ? 'Click an expert to split its gate, up and down matrices.' : 'Click a label or marker. Full inventory stays in the inspector.'}</div>`}
 <div class="scene-tools"><button class=${'icon-button ' + (s.labels ? 'active' : '')} aria-label="Toggle all layer labels" aria-pressed=${s.labels} onClick=${ () => this.patch({
                            labels: !s.labels
                        })} title="Layer labels (L)"><${Icon} name="labels"/></button><button class=${'icon-button ' + (s.links ? 'active' : '')} aria-label="Toggle flow paths" aria-pressed=${s.links} onClick=${ () => this.patch({
                            links: !s.links
                        })} title="Flow paths (K)"><${Icon} name="link"/></button><button class="icon-button" aria-label="Reset camera" onClick=${ () => this.engine && this.engine.reset()} title="Reset view (R)"><${Icon} name="reset"/></button><button class="icon-button" aria-label="Save 3D canvas image" onClick=${ () => this.snapshot()} title="Save canvas image"><${Icon} name="camera"/></button></div>
 <div class="scene-scale"><span class="scale-glyph"/><span>1 cubic unit = 1 GB <em>${s.view === 'cache' ? 'global KV payload' : 'modeled weight payload'} / ${this.engine && this.engine.renderer.software ? 'CPU 3D' : 'WebGL2'}</em></span></div><div class="scene-legend">${(s.view === 'cache' ? [['full', 'Encoder KV'], ['dec', 'Decoder KV']] : s.view === 'storage' ? [['expert', 'Experts'], ['engram', 'Engram'], ['attn', 'Attention']] : [['full', 'Full'], ['reindex', 'Reindex'], ['reuse', 'Reuse'], ['swa', 'SWA only']]).map( ([key,l]) => html`<span><i style=${{
                            background: COL[key]
                        }}/>${l}</span>`)}</div>
 ${s.view === 'architecture' && s.activity === 'inference' && (phaseI === 3 || s.selected && s.selected[0] === 'D') && html`<div class="draft-diagram"><div><b>DSPARK / FIVE-TOKEN DRAFT</b><small>Synthetic confidence example</small></div><div class="draft-cells">${[.98, .94, .87, .62, .41].map( (confidence, i) => {
                            let step = (s.time >= 16 ? s.time - 16 : 2)
                              , stage = step < 1.2 ? 'proposed' : step < 2.8 ? 'scored' : i < 3 ? 'accepted' : i === 3 ? 'corrected' : 'redraft';
                            return html`<div class=${'draft-cell ' + stage}><small>t + ${i + 1}</small><b>${step < 1.2 ? '...' : confidence.toFixed(2)}</b><span>${stage}</span></div>`;
                        }
                        )}</div><p>Token states, not weight volumes. No measured acceptance rate.</p></div>`}
 ${nv && s.view !== 'cache' && html`<div class="format-notice"><b>+${bytes(NV_DELTA, s.binary)}</b> modeled vs native / smaller scale groups</div>`}${s.error && html`<div class="graphics-error"><h2>Graphics unavailable</h2><p>${s.error}</p><p>The inspector, configs, benchmarks and byte audit still work. Enable canvas rendering and reload for the 3D scene.</p></div>`}
 </section>
 <div class=${'transport-shell' + (s.timelineHidden ? ' collapsed' : '')}><footer id="animation-panel" class="transport" inert=${s.timelineHidden} aria-hidden=${s.timelineHidden} onPointerMove=${e => this.blankMove(e, 'down')} onPointerLeave=${e => e.currentTarget.classList.remove('blank-down')} onPointerDown=${e => this.blankDown(e, 'down')} onPointerUp=${e => this.blankUp(e, 'down')}><button class="timeline-collapse" aria-label="Hide animation bar" title="Hide animation bar (T), or click its blank background" onClick=${ () => this.patch({
                            timelineHidden: true
                        })}><${Icon} name="down" size=${15}/></button><div class="story-text"><div><span class="story-phase" style=${{
                            color: phase.color
                        }}>0${phaseI + 1} / ${phase.name.toUpperCase()}</span><b>${phase.caption}</b></div><span class="story-number" style=${{
                            color: phase.color
                        }}>${phase.active}<small>${s.activity === 'training' ? 'TRAINING SCHEMATIC' : phaseI === 0 || phaseI === 2 ? 'ACTIVE / TOKEN' : 'SCHEMATIC STEP'}</small></span></div><div class="story-description">${phase.desc}</div><div class="transport-row"><button class="play-button" aria-label=${s.playing ? 'Pause animation' : 'Play animation'} onClick=${ () => this.patch({
                            playing: !s.playing
                        })}><${Icon} name=${s.playing ? 'pause' : 'play'} size=${18}/></button><div class="timeline"><div class="phase-buttons">${phases.map( (p, i) => html`<button class=${phaseI === i ? 'active' : ''} style=${{
                            '--phase': p.color,
                            flex: p.to - p.from
                        }} onClick=${ () => this.seek(p.from + .01)}>${p.label || p.name}</button>`)}</div><input aria-label="Animation timeline" type="range" min="0" max="21.99" step="0.01" value=${s.time} onInput=${e => this.seek(Number(e.target.value))} style=${{
                            '--progress': s.time / 22 * 100 + '%'
                        }}/></div><button class=${'phase-loop ' + (s.loopPhase ? 'active' : '')} aria-label="Loop current animation phase" aria-pressed=${s.loopPhase} title="Stay on this phase, or play the whole request story" onClick=${ () => this.patch({
                            loopPhase: !s.loopPhase
                        })}>${s.loopPhase ? 'Loop phase' : 'All phases'}</button><select class="speed-select" value=${s.speed} aria-label="Animation speed" onChange=${e => this.patch({
                            speed: Number(e.target.value)
                        })}>${[.5, 1, 1.5, 2].map(v => html`<option value=${v}>${v}x</option>`)}</select><button class=${'orbit-button ' + (s.orbit ? 'active' : '')} aria-label="Toggle camera auto orbit" aria-pressed=${s.orbit} onClick=${ () => this.patch({
                            orbit: !s.orbit
                        })}>Orbit</button></div><div class="transport-bottom"><span>${s.activity === 'training' ? 'GENERIC TRAINING SCHEMATIC / NO REAL WEIGHT UPDATES' : phase.name === 'Prefill' ? '* OPTIMIZED PREFILL / NOT THE REFERENCE FORWARD LOOP' : 'SCHEMATIC FLOW / NOT MEASURED TIMING'}</span><span>drag orbit / right-drag pan / wheel zoom</span></div></footer></div>
 <div class=${'timeline-dock' + (s.timelineHidden ? ' mini' : '')}><button aria-label=${s.timelineHidden ? 'Show animation bar' : 'Hide animation bar'} aria-expanded=${!s.timelineHidden} aria-controls="animation-panel" onClick=${ () => this.patch({
                            timelineHidden: !s.timelineHidden
                        })}><${Icon} name=${s.timelineHidden ? 'up' : 'down'} size=${14}/><span>${s.timelineHidden ? 'Animation / ' + phase.name : 'Hide animation'}</span></button>${s.timelineHidden && html`<div class="mini-playback"><button aria-label=${s.playing ? 'Pause animation' : 'Play animation'} onClick=${ () => this.patch({
                            playing: !s.playing
                        })}><${Icon} name=${s.playing ? 'pause' : 'play'} size=${14}/></button><select aria-label="Animation phase" value=${phaseI} onChange=${e => this.seek(phases[+e.target.value].from + .01)}>${phases.map( (p, i) => html`<option value=${i}>${p.label || p.name}</option>`)}</select></div>`}<span class="weight-state">${s.activity === 'training' ? 'Training schematic / sizes unchanged' : phase.name === 'Decode' ? 'Decode / all 40 layers' : phase.name === 'Prefill' ? 'Optimized prompt prefill' : 'Inference / weights stay fixed'}</span></div>
 </main>${this.renderInspector()}${(s.panelHidden || s.mobile && !s.sheet) && html`<button class="inspector-reopen" aria-label="Show inspector" aria-controls="inspector-panel" onClick=${ () => this.togglePanel(true)} title="Show inspector (B)"><${Icon} name="menu" size=${16}/><span>Inspect</span></button>`}</div>${s.mobile && s.sheet && html`<div class="sheet-scrim" onClick=${ () => this.patch({
                            sheet: false
                        })}/>`}${this.renderModal()}${s.toast && html`<div class="toast" role="status">${s.toast}</div>`}</div>`;
                    }
                }
                render(html`<${App}/>`, document.getElementById('root'));

            }
            )();
        </script>
    </body>
</html>
